/**
 * Lemonade Stand — MBA skills reports for groups (classes and competitions).
 * Reads groups, members and decision logs from Firestore and writes PDFs to Google Drive:
 *   Lemonade Stand Archive / Reports / <College> / <Group (CODE)> / <Student (Player ID)>.pdf
 *   … plus "Group summary" in each group folder, "College summary" in each college folder,
 *   and the spreadsheet "Lemonade Stand reports" (Students and Groups tabs) with a link to every PDF.
 * Run reports() any time (it only redoes PDFs with new data), or reportsAll() to redo everything; long runs continue by themselves.
 * setup() in Code.gs also runs it every Monday morning. Uses api(), val(), BASE and FOLDER from Code.gs.
 * Covers decisions still in Firebase (the last 180 days); older days are in the monthly archive sheets.
 */
var REPORTS_FOLDER = 'Reports';
var CUR_INFO = { USD: [1, '$'], EUR: [1, '€'], GBP: [1, '£'], CAD: [1, 'C$'], AUD: [2, 'A$'], CHF: [2, 'CHF '], INR: [50, '₹'], CNY: [5, 'CN¥'],
  JPY: [100, '¥'], KRW: [1000, '₩'], IDR: [10000, 'Rp '], BRL: [5, 'R$'], MXN: [20, 'MX$'], NGN: [1000, '₦'] };

// reportsAll() redoes every PDF. A big class may need several 6-minute runs: each run stops safely at 4.5 minutes,
// remembers which PDFs are done, and schedules reportsContinue() a minute later until everything is written.
function reportsAll() { var p = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); p.setProperty('rep_force', String(Date.now())); p.deleteProperty('rep_cycle'); reports(); }
function reportsContinue() { reports(); }
var REP_CUTOFF = new Date(8.64e15);   // set by reports(): the start of the current round

function reports() {   // takes no arguments: a trigger passes an event object, which must not mean "redo everything"
  var started = Date.now(), props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), force = props.getProperty('rep_force') || '0';
  // One round of reports uses the data as it was when the round began, so students playing meanwhile cannot keep it
  // running forever; their newer days go into the next round.
  var cycle = props.getProperty('rep_cycle');
  if (!cycle || started - Number(cycle) > 24 * 3600 * 1000) { cycle = String(started); props.setProperty('rep_cycle', cycle); }   // a stuck round never freezes data for long
  REP_CUTOFF = new Date(Number(cycle));
  // Firestore reads are rationed (50,000 a day on the free plan): a round reads Firestore once and keeps a gzipped copy
  // in Drive, so its continuation batches read nothing from Firestore.
  var snapFresh = !loadSnap(cycle);
  try { reportsRun(started, props, force, snapFresh, cycle); }
  catch (err) {
    if (String(err).indexOf('Firestore 429') < 0) throw err;
    Logger.log('Firestore daily read limit reached. Reports will continue by themselves at 1:45 pm India time, after it resets.');
    ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'reportsContinue') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
    var at = new Date(); at.setUTCHours(8, 15, 0, 0); if (at <= new Date()) at.setUTCDate(at.getUTCDate() + 1);   // 1:45 pm IST, after the quota resets
    ScriptApp.newTrigger('reportsContinue').timeBased().at(at).create();
  }
}

function reportsRun(started, props, force, snapFresh, cycle) {
  var outOfTime = function () { return Date.now() - started > 4.5 * 60 * 1000; };
  var groups = query('groups', null), byCollege = {}, rows = [], groupRows = [], cache = {}, done = 0, left = 0, changed = false, MOD = readModeration();
  groups.forEach(function (gd) {
    var code = gd.name.split('/').pop(), f = gd.fields || {};
    var g = { code: code, name: val(f.name), college: val(f.college) || 'No college', batch: val(f.batch), ownerName: val(f.ownerName) };
    var members = query('members', { field: 'code', op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: code } }).map(function (d) {
      var mf = d.fields || {}; return { pid: val(mf.pid), name: val(mf.name), college: val(mf.college), t: new Date(val(mf.t)) };
    });
    if (!members.length) return;
    var students = members.map(function (mb) { return studentReport(mb, cache); });
    var folder = path([REPORTS_FOLDER, g.college, g.name + ' (' + code + ')']);
    sortStudents(students);
    // A student's PDF changes when anyone's days change (ranks and class averages), so the group's data is in each stamp.
    var gStamp = 'g3|' + force + '|' + students.map(function (s) { return s.pid + ':' + s.att.map(function (a) { return a.days.length; }).join('.'); }).join('|');
    var gLeft = 0;
    students.forEach(function (s, i) {
      /* rank set by sortStudents */
      var key = 'rs_' + code + '_' + s.pid, name = s.name + ' (' + s.pid + ')';
      s.mod = MOD[code + '|' + s.pid] || { m: '', c: '' };
      var stamp = gStamp + '|' + s.mod.m + '|' + s.mod.c;   // a new moderated mark or comment redoes this PDF
      if (props.getProperty(key) === stamp) s.url = fileUrl(folder, name);
      else if (outOfTime()) { gLeft++; s.url = fileUrl(folder, name); }
      else { s.url = savePdf(folder, name, studentHtml(s, g, students)); props.setProperty(key, stamp); done++; changed = true; }
      rows.push([new Date(), g.college, g.name, code, g.batch, s.name, s.pid, s.days.length, s.restarts].concat(s.mk ? [s.mk.marks, s.mk.gr.g, s.mk.gr.gp]
        .concat(AREA_ORDER.map(function (a) { var x = areaOf(s, a); return x ? x.m : ''; })) : ['', 'Not graded (under 5 days)', ''].concat(AREA_ORDER.map(function () { return ''; }))).concat([s.url, s.mod.m, s.mod.c]));
    });
    var gUrl;
    if (props.getProperty('rep_' + code) === gStamp) gUrl = fileUrl(folder, 'Group summary');
    else if (gLeft || outOfTime()) { gLeft++; gUrl = fileUrl(folder, 'Group summary'); }
    else { gUrl = savePdf(folder, 'Group summary', groupHtml(g, students)); props.setProperty('rep_' + code, gStamp); changed = true; }
    left += gLeft;
    var st = classStats(students);
    groupRows.push([g.college, g.name, code, g.batch, g.ownerName, students.length, st.n, st.n ? st.mean : '', st.n ? st.pass : '', gUrl]);
    (byCollege[g.college] = byCollege[g.college] || []).push({ g: g, students: students });
  });
  if (!left && (changed || props.getProperty('rep_college') !== force))
    Object.keys(byCollege).forEach(function (c) { savePdf(path([REPORTS_FOLDER, c]), 'College summary', collegeHtml(c, byCollege[c])); });
  if (!left) { props.setProperty('rep_college', force); props.deleteProperty('rep_cycle'); }
  if (snapFresh && left) saveSnap(cycle);
  writeIndex(rows, groupRows);
  ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function (t) { if (t.getHandlerFunction() === 'reportsContinue') ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
  if (left) ScriptApp.newTrigger('reportsContinue').timeBased().after(60 * 1000).create();
  Logger.log('Groups: ' + groups.length + ', student PDFs written: ' + done + (left ? ', still to do: ' + left + ' (continuing automatically in about a minute)' : ', all reports up to date'));
}

/* ---------- Firestore ---------- */
var SNAP = null;   // this round's copy of every Firestore query: key -> documents
function query(collection, filter) {
  var key = collection + '|' + (filter ? filter.field + '=' + filter.value.stringValue : '');
  if (SNAP && SNAP[key]) return SNAP[key];
  var docs = queryFirestore(collection, filter);
  if (SNAP) SNAP[key] = docs;
  return docs;
}
function snapFile() { var f = path([REPORTS_FOLDER]).getFilesByName('report-data-snapshot.gz'); return f.hasNext() ? f.next() : null; }
function loadSnap(cycle) {   // true when this round's saved copy was loaded
  SNAP = {};
  var f = snapFile(); if (!f) return false;
  try { var o = JSON.parse(Utilities.ungzip(f.getBlob().setContentType('application/x-gzip')).getDataAsString()); if (o.cycle === cycle) { SNAP = o.snap; return true; } } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}
function saveSnap(cycle) {
  var old = snapFile(); if (old) old.setTrashed(true);
  path([REPORTS_FOLDER]).createFile(Utilities.gzip(Utilities.newBlob(JSON.stringify({ cycle: cycle, snap: SNAP }), 'application/json'), 'report-data-snapshot.gz'));
}
function queryFirestore(collection, filter) {
  var q = { from: [{ collectionId: collection }] };
  if (filter) q.where = { fieldFilter: { field: { fieldPath: filter.field }, op: filter.op, value: filter.value } };
  return api('post', BASE + ':runQuery', { structuredQuery: q }).filter(function (x) { return x.document; }).map(function (x) { return x.document; });
}
/* All decision days a member saved since joining the group (research records are batches of days). */
function studentReport(mb, cache) {
  if (!cache[mb.pid]) cache[mb.pid] = query('research', { field: 'pid', op: 'EQUAL', value: { stringValue: mb.pid } })
    .map(function (d) { var f = d.fields || {}, days = []; try { days = JSON.parse(val(f.days) || '[]'); } catch (e) {} return { t: new Date(val(f.t)), days: days }; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return a.t - b.t; });
  var days = [];
  cache[mb.pid].forEach(function (r) { if (r.t >= mb.t && r.t <= REP_CUTOFF) days = days.concat(r.days); });
  // Attempts rule (grading v2): every game started after joining the group is an attempt. A game restarted while the
  // stand still had money is ABANDONED and ignored; one that ended out of money is FAILED (−5 marks each, at most −15).
  // The best attempt of 5 or more days is graded. The latest game under 5 days is in progress and not counted yet.
  var games = []; days.forEach(function (d) { if (games.indexOf(d.g) < 0) games.push(d.g); });
  var cur = days.length ? days[days.length - 1].cur || 'INR' : 'INR';
  var att = games.map(function (g, i) {
    var dd = days.filter(function (d) { return d.g === g; }), k = SK.kpis(dd), sk = SK.skills(k), cs = sk ? conceptScores(dd, k, cur) : null;
    var current = i === games.length - 1, last = dd[dd.length - 1];
    var status = current ? (dd.length < MIN_DAYS ? 'progress' : 'current') : (wentBroke(dd) ? 'failed' : 'abandoned');
    return { n: i + 1, days: dd, k: k, sk: sk, cs: cs, mk: cs && cs.list.length && dd.length >= MIN_DAYS ? marksFor(cs, dd) : null, current: current, status: status };
  });
  var failed = att.filter(function (a) { return a.status === 'failed'; }).length;
  var detail = att.filter(function (a) { return a.mk; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.mk.marks - a.mk.marks || b.days.length - a.days.length; })[0] ||
    att[att.length - 1] || { n: 0, days: [], k: SK.kpis([]), sk: null, cs: null, mk: null };
  var mk = null;
  if (detail.mk) {
    var pen = Math.min(15, 5 * failed), fin = r1(Math.max(0, detail.mk.marks - pen));
    mk = {}; Object.keys(detail.mk).forEach(function (x) { mk[x] = detail.mk[x]; });
    mk.caps = detail.mk.caps.slice(); mk.attempt = detail.mk.marks; mk.failed = failed; mk.failPen = pen; mk.marks = fin; mk.gr = gradeOf(fin);
    if (pen) mk.caps.push(failed + ' failed game' + (failed > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' (ran out of money): −' + pen);
    mk.inc = !REP_FINAL && detail.days.length < EVIDENCE_DAYS && fin < 40;
    if (mk.inc) mk.gr = INC;
  }
  return { pid: mb.pid, name: mb.name || mb.pid, college: mb.college, days: detail.days, k: detail.k, sk: detail.sk, cur: cur, cs: detail.cs, mk: mk,
    att: att, detailN: detail.n, games: games.length, restarts: Math.max(0, games.length - 1), failed: failed };
}
/* A game ended out of money: the last day's cash cannot buy a pitcher and net worth is nearly gone, or the student went to jail. */
function wentBroke(dd) {
  var l = dd[dd.length - 1]; if (!l) return false;
  return (l.cash < 200 && l.nw < 2000) || dd.some(function (d, i) { return d.sz > 0 && i > 0 && dd[i - 1].bk > 0 && dd[i - 1].rj > 0; });
}

/* ---------- Drive ---------- */
function path(parts) {
  var it = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(FOLDER), f = it.hasNext() ? it.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(FOLDER);
  parts.forEach(function (p) { p = String(p).replace(/[\/\\]/g, '-').slice(0, 100); var s = f.getFoldersByName(p); f = s.hasNext() ? s.next() : f.createFolder(p); });
  return f;
}
function savePdf(folder, name, html) {
  var blob = Utilities.newBlob(html, MimeType.HTML, name + '.html').getAs(MimeType.PDF).setName(name + '.pdf');
  var old = folder.getFilesByName(name + '.pdf');
  if (old.hasNext()) {   // replace the contents in place, so links already opened or shared show the latest report
    var f = old.next();
    UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files/' + f.getId() + '?uploadType=media', { method: 'patch', contentType: 'application/pdf',
      payload: blob.getBytes(), headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer ' + ScriptApp.getOAuthToken() } });
    while (old.hasNext()) old.next().setTrashed(true);
    return f.getUrl();
  }
  return folder.createFile(blob).getUrl();
}
function fileUrl(folder, name) { var f = folder.getFilesByName(name + '.pdf'); return f.hasNext() ? f.next().getUrl() : ''; }
/* Faculty moderation typed into the index sheet (Students tab): kept across runs and printed in the student's PDF. */
function readModeration() {
  var out = {}, files = path([REPORTS_FOLDER]).getFilesByName('Lemonade Stand reports');
  if (!files.hasNext()) return out;
  var sh = SpreadsheetApp.open(files.next()).getSheetByName('Students'); if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return out;
  var v = sh.getDataRange().getValues(), h = v[0], ci = h.indexOf('Code'), pi = h.indexOf('Player ID'), mi = h.indexOf('Moderated marks %'), fi = h.indexOf('Faculty comment');
  if (ci < 0 || pi < 0 || (mi < 0 && fi < 0)) return out;
  v.slice(1).forEach(function (r) {
    var m = mi < 0 ? '' : r[mi], c = fi < 0 ? '' : String(r[fi] || '').trim();
    m = m === '' || m === null || isNaN(Number(m)) ? '' : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(Number(m) * 10) / 10));
    if (m !== '' || c) out[r[ci] + '|' + r[pi]] = { m: m, c: c.slice(0, 1000) };
  });
  return out;
}
function writeIndex(rows, groupRows) {
  var root = path([REPORTS_FOLDER]), files = root.getFilesByName('Lemonade Stand reports'), ss;
  if (files.hasNext()) ss = SpreadsheetApp.open(files.next());
  else { ss = SpreadsheetApp.create('Lemonade Stand reports'); DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId()).moveTo(root); ss.getSheets()[0].setName('Students'); ss.insertSheet('Groups'); }
  var put = function (name, head, data) {
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name); sh.clear(); sh.appendRow(head); sh.setFrozenRows(1); sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setFontWeight('bold');
    if (data.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, data.length, head.length).setValues(data);
  };
  put('Students', ['Updated', 'College', 'Group', 'Code', 'Batch', 'Student', 'Player ID', 'Days graded', 'Restarts', 'Marks %', 'Grade', 'Grade points'].concat(AREA_ORDER).concat(['Report PDF', 'Moderated marks %', 'Faculty comment']), rows);
  put('Groups', ['College', 'Group', 'Code', 'Batch', 'Created by', 'Students', 'Graded', 'Average marks %', 'Pass rate', 'Group summary PDF'], groupRows);
}

/* ---------- PDF pages ---------- */
var CSS = '<style>body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:10pt;color:#1f2937}h1{font-size:17pt;color:#9a6700;margin:0}h2{font-size:12pt;margin:16px 0 6px;color:#374151}' +
  'table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}td,th{border:1px solid #d1d5db;padding:5px 6px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}th{background:#fef3c7}' +
  '.big{font-size:22pt;font-weight:bold;color:#15803d}.muted{color:#6b7280;font-size:8.5pt}.n{text-align:right}.box{border:1px solid #9ca3af;height:90px}</style>';
function e(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]; }); }
function money(gc, cur) {
  var c = CUR_INFO[cur] || CUR_INFO.INR, d = c[0] <= 5 ? 2 : 0;   // cents for dollar-like currencies
  return gc == null ? '—' : (gc < 0 ? '−' : '') + c[1] + (Math.abs(gc) / 100 * c[0]).toLocaleString('en-IN', { minimumFractionDigits: d, maximumFractionDigits: d });
}
function pc(x) { return x == null ? '—' : Math.round(x * 100) + '%'; }
function stars(s) { var n = Math.round(s / 20); return '★★★★★'.slice(0, n) + '☆☆☆☆☆'.slice(n); }
function avgOf(a, f) { return a.length ? a.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + f(x); }, 0) / a.length : 0; }
function head(title, sub) { return '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8">' + CSS + '</head><body><h1>🍋 ' + e(title) + '</h1><div class="muted">' + sub + '</div>'; }
var FOOT = '<p class="muted">Lemonade Stand: Business Sim · reports by Dr. Govindraj R. Mane, Department of MBA, KLS Gogte Institute of Technology, Belagavi. ' +
  'Marks are computed from the student\'s own game decisions.</p></body></html>';
/* ---------- Grading rules, version 2 (agreed 21 Nov 2026; see the "Grading Rules (Version 2)" document) ----------
 * Graded attempt marks out of 100:
 *   A. Management concepts (65): each concept 0–100 from the student's decisions; area = plain average of its concepts
 *      (no squaring); areas weighted Finance 14, Operations 12, Marketing 12, Economics 8, Strategy 8, People 3, Ethics 3;
 *      an area never met is left out and its weight shared by the others.
 *   B. Business results (25): average daily profit (15) and net worth growth a day (10), each against a benchmark per
 *      difficulty (Easy ₹1,500, Normal ₹1,000, Hard ₹700 a day), straight line from ₹0.
 *   C. Persistence (10): days played, 0 at 5 days, full at 30.
 * (A + B) × evidence factor (days ÷ 15, at most 1) + C, then: −3 per bribe (at most −15); 5+ bribes → at most 49;
 * equipment seized → at most 49; jail → 0. Then −5 per failed game (at most −15, in studentReport). Never below 0.
 * Incomplete: graded attempt under 15 days and marks below 40, until the group's reports are marked Final. */
var GRADES = [[90, 'S', 10], [80, 'A', 9], [70, 'B', 8], [60, 'C', 7], [50, 'D', 6], [40, 'E', 4], [0, 'F', 0]];
var GCOL = { S: '#166534', A: '#15803d', B: '#2563eb', C: '#0e7490', D: '#b45309', E: '#c2410c', F: '#b91c1c', I: '#6b7280' };
var INC = { g: 'I', gp: 0 };
var AREA_ORDER = ['Finance and accounting', 'Operations and supply chain', 'Marketing and pricing', 'Economics and decision-making', 'Strategy and growth', 'People and organisation', 'Ethics and compliance'];
var AREA_W = { 'Finance and accounting': 14, 'Operations and supply chain': 12, 'Marketing and pricing': 12, 'Economics and decision-making': 8, 'Strategy and growth': 8, 'People and organisation': 3, 'Ethics and compliance': 3 };
var BENCH = { easy: 3000, normal: 2000, hard: 1400 };   // game cents a day (₹1 = 2 G¢): ₹1,500, ₹1,000, ₹700
var EVIDENCE_DAYS = 15, MIN_DAYS = 5, FULL_DAYS = 30;
var REP_FINAL = false;   // the group's reports are marked Final: Incomplete students get their calculated grade
function r1(x) { return Math.round(x * 10) / 10; }
function gradeOf(m) { for (var i = 0; i < GRADES.length; i++) if (m >= GRADES[i][0]) return { g: GRADES[i][1], gp: GRADES[i][2] }; return { g: 'F', gp: 0 }; }
function clamp01(x) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x)); }
function marksFor(cs, days) {
  cs.list.forEach(function (c) { c.m = c.s; c.gr = gradeOf(c.s); });
  var areas = AREA_ORDER.map(function (a) {
    var l = cs.list.filter(function (c) { return c.area === a; }); if (!l.length) return null;
    var m = r1(avgOf(l, function (c) { return c.s; })); return { area: a, m: m, gr: gradeOf(m), n: l.length, raw: Math.round(m), w: AREA_W[a] };
  }).filter(Boolean);
  var ws = areas.reduce(function (t, a) { return t + a.w; }, 0), A = ws ? areas.reduce(function (t, a) { return t + a.m * a.w; }, 0) / ws * 0.65 : 0;
  var n = days.length, first = days[0], last = days[n - 1], dif = first.dif || 'normal', ben = BENCH[dif] || BENCH.normal;
  var avgP = days.reduce(function (t, d) { return t + (d.np || 0); }, 0) / n, gpd = (last.nw - (first.nw - first.np)) / n;
  var B1 = 15 * clamp01(avgP / ben), B2 = 10 * clamp01(gpd / ben), C = 10 * clamp01((n - MIN_DAYS) / (FULL_DAYS - MIN_DAYS));
  var ev = Math.min(1, n / EVIDENCE_DAYS), base = (A + B1 + B2) * ev + C, caps = [];
  var bribes = days.filter(function (d) { return d.of === 'bribe'; }).length, penB = Math.min(15, 3 * bribes), marks = base - penB;
  var seized = days.some(function (d) { return d.sz > 0; }), jail = days.some(function (d, i) { return d.sz > 0 && i > 0 && days[i - 1].bk > 0 && days[i - 1].rj > 0; });
  if (ev < 1) caps.push('Evidence factor: ' + n + ' of ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days, so Parts A and B × ' + ev.toFixed(2));
  if (bribes) caps.push(bribes + ' bribe' + (bribes > 1 ? 's' : '') + ': −' + penB);
  if (bribes >= 5 && marks > 49) { marks = 49; caps.push('Five or more bribes: marks capped at 49'); }
  if (seized && marks > 49) { marks = 49; caps.push('Lost equipment to a lender: marks capped at 49'); }
  if (jail) { marks = 0; caps.push('Went to jail (bank and moneylender default): 0 marks'); }
  marks = r1(Math.max(0, marks));
  return { A: r1(A), B1: r1(B1), B2: r1(B2), B: r1(B1 + B2), C: r1(C), ev: ev, base: r1(base), bribes: bribes, penB: penB, seized: seized, jail: jail,
    avgP: avgP, gpd: gpd, ben: ben, dif: dif, raw: r1(A / 0.65), marks: marks, gr: gradeOf(marks), areas: areas, caps: caps };
}
/* Graded students by marks (Incomplete after them, then not graded), with their rank among the graded ones. */
function sortStudents(students) {
  var key = function (s) { return s.mk ? (s.mk.inc ? -1 : s.mk.marks) : -2; };
  students.sort(function (a, b) { return key(b) - key(a) || (b.mk ? b.mk.marks : 0) - (a.mk ? a.mk.marks : 0); });
  var r = 0; students.forEach(function (s) { s.rank = s.mk && !s.mk.inc ? ++r : null; });
  return students;
}
function areaOf(s, a) { return s.mk ? s.mk.areas.filter(function (x) { return x.area === a; })[0] : null; }
function classStats(students) {
  var v = students.filter(function (s) { return s.mk && !s.mk.inc; }).map(function (s) { return s.mk.marks; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }), n = v.length;
  if (!n) return { n: 0 };
  var mean = avgOf(v, function (x) { return x; }), sd = Math.sqrt(avgOf(v, function (x) { return (x - mean) * (x - mean); }));
  var dist = {}; GRADES.forEach(function (g) { dist[g[1]] = 0; }); v.forEach(function (x) { dist[gradeOf(x).g]++; });
  return { n: n, mean: r1(mean), median: r1(n % 2 ? v[(n - 1) / 2] : (v[n / 2 - 1] + v[n / 2]) / 2), max: v[n - 1], min: v[0], sd: r1(sd),
    pass: Math.round(100 * v.filter(function (x) { return x >= 40; }).length / n) + '%', gp: r1(avgOf(v, function (x) { return gradeOf(x).gp; })), dist: dist };
}
/* ---------- Charts. Google's HTML→PDF conversion drops all background colours, so bars are coloured █ characters
 * and trend charts are PNG images from the built-in Charts service (both tested in a real conversion). ---------- */
function gbadge(gr) { return '<b style="color:' + GCOL[gr.g] + '">' + gr.g + '</b>'; }
function hbar(pct, colour, n) {   // n blocks wide (default 25, so one block = 4%)
  n = n || 25; var k = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(100, pct)) / 100 * n);
  return '<span style="color:' + colour + ';letter-spacing:-1px">' + new Array(k + 1).join('█') + '</span><span style="color:#e5e7eb;letter-spacing:-1px">' + new Array(n - k + 1).join('█') + '</span>';
}
function hbars(rows) {   // rows: [label, value 0–100, text, colour]
  return '<table style="border:0">' + rows.map(function (r) {
    return '<tr><td style="border:0;width:34%;padding:1px 4px">' + e(r[0]) + '</td><td style="border:0;padding:1px 0;white-space:nowrap">' + hbar(r[1], r[3]) + '</td>' +
      '<td style="border:0;width:16%;padding:1px 4px;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap">' + r[2] + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>';
}
function chartImg(kind, rows, colour, yTitle, w, h) {   // rows: [[label, number], ...]
  try {
    var dt = Charts.newDataTable().addColumn(Charts.ColumnType.STRING, 'x').addColumn(Charts.ColumnType.NUMBER, yTitle || 'y');
    rows.forEach(function (r) { dt.addRow([String(r[0]), r[1]]); });
    var c = (kind === 'line' ? Charts.newLineChart() : Charts.newColumnChart()).setDataTable(dt.build()).setColors([colour]).setLegendPosition(Charts.Position.NONE)
      .setDimensions(w || 680, h || 220).setYAxisTitle(yTitle || '').build();
    return '<img src="data:image/png;base64,' + Utilities.base64Encode(c.getAs('image/png').getBytes()) + '" width="' + (w || 680) + '">';
  } catch (err) { return '<p class="muted">(Chart could not be drawn: ' + e(String(err)) + ')</p>'; }
}
function rupees(gc, cur) { var c = CUR_INFO[cur] || CUR_INFO.INR; return Math.round(gc / 100 * c[0] * 100) / 100; }
function gradeHistogram(dist) {
  return chartImg('column', GRADES.map(function (g) { return [g[1], dist[g[1]]]; }), '#2563eb', 'Students', 520, 200) +
    '<table style="width:70%"><tr>' + GRADES.map(function (g) { return '<th style="text-align:center;color:' + GCOL[g[1]] + '">' + g[1] + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr><tr>' +
    GRADES.map(function (g) { return '<td style="text-align:center">' + dist[g[1]] + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr></table>';
}

function collegeHtml(college, list) {
  var h = head('College Summary', e(college) + ' · ' + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Kolkata', 'd MMM yyyy')), everyone = [];
  list.forEach(function (x) { x.students.filter(function (s) { return s.mk; }).forEach(function (s) { everyone.push({ s: s, g: x.g }); }); });
  var all = classStats(everyone.map(function (x) { return x.s; }));
  if (all.n) h += '<h2>All graded students: ' + all.n + '</h2><table><tr><th>Mean marks</th><th>Median</th><th>Highest</th><th>Lowest</th><th>Std. deviation</th><th>Pass rate</th><th>Mean grade points</th></tr><tr>' +
    [all.mean + '%', all.median + '%', all.max + '%', all.min + '%', all.sd, all.pass, all.gp].map(function (x) { return '<td class="n"><b>' + x + '</b></td>'; }).join('') + '</tr></table><h2>Grade distribution</h2>' + gradeHistogram(all.dist);
  h += '<h2>Groups</h2><table><tr><th>Group</th><th>Code</th><th>Batch</th><th>Joined</th><th>Graded</th><th>Mean marks</th><th>Pass rate</th><th>S · A · B · C · D · E · F</th></tr>' + list.map(function (x) {
    var st = classStats(x.students);
    return '<tr><td>' + e(x.g.name) + '</td><td>' + x.g.code + '</td><td>' + e(x.g.batch) + '</td><td class="n">' + x.students.length + '</td><td class="n">' + st.n + '</td><td class="n"><b>' + (st.n ? st.mean + '%' : '—') + '</b></td><td class="n">' + (st.n ? st.pass : '—') + '</td><td>' + (st.n ? GRADES.map(function (gg) { return st.dist[gg[1]]; }).join(' · ') : '—') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>';
  if (list.length > 1) h += '<h2>Mean marks by group</h2>' + hbars(list.map(function (x) { var st = classStats(x.students); return st.n ? [x.g.name, st.mean, st.mean + '% ' + gbadge(gradeOf(st.mean)), GCOL[gradeOf(st.mean).g]] : null; }).filter(Boolean));
  var ar = AREA_ORDER.map(function (a) { var v = everyone.map(function (x) { return areaOf(x.s, a); }).filter(Boolean).map(function (x) { return x.m; }); return v.length ? [a, r1(avgOf(v, function (x) { return x; }))] : null; }).filter(Boolean);
  if (ar.length) h += '<h2>Area-wise performance (college average)</h2>' + hbars(ar.map(function (x) { var gr = gradeOf(x[1]); return [x[0], x[1], x[1] + '% ' + gbadge(gr), GCOL[gr.g]]; }));
  everyone.sort(function (a, b) { return b.s.mk.marks - a.s.mk.marks; });
  h += '<h2>Top 10 students</h2><table><tr><th>#</th><th>Student</th><th>Group</th><th>Marks</th><th>Grade</th><th>GP</th></tr>' +
    everyone.slice(0, 10).map(function (x, i) { return '<tr><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td>' + e(x.s.name) + '</td><td>' + e(x.g.name) + '</td><td class="n">' + x.s.mk.marks + '%</td><td>' + gbadge(x.s.mk.gr) + '</td><td class="n">' + x.s.mk.gr.gp + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>';
  return h + FOOT;
}

/* ---------- Concept-by-concept scores: each MBA concept, what it is, how the game uses it, and what the student did ----------
 * conceptScores(days, k, cur) → [{ area, name, what, applied, s (0–100), good, bad, tip }] for concepts seen in play,
 * plus .missing: names of concepts the student never practised. Scores are rules of thumb for feedback, not exam marks. */
var WX = { hot: [1.20, 4], sunny: [1.00, 3], cloudy: [0.95, 2], cool: [0.85, 1], rainy: [0.85, 1], stormy: [0.80, 1] };   // [fair-price factor, ideal ice]
function conceptScores(days, k, cur) {
  var O = days.filter(function (d) { return !d.cl; }), out = [], missing = [];
  var cl = function (x) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x)); }, P = function (x) { return Math.round(x * 100) + '%'; }, M = function (x) { return money(x, cur); };
  var sum = function (a, f) { return a.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + f(x); }, 0); }, avg = function (a, f) { return a.length ? sum(a, f) / a.length : 0; };
  var share = function (a, f) { return a.length ? a.filter(f).length / a.length : 0; };
  var corr = function (pts) {
    if (pts.length < 3) return null;
    var mx = avg(pts, function (p) { return p[0]; }), my = avg(pts, function (p) { return p[1]; });
    var sxy = sum(pts, function (p) { return (p[0] - mx) * (p[1] - my); }), sxx = sum(pts, function (p) { return (p[0] - mx) * (p[0] - mx); }), syy = sum(pts, function (p) { return (p[1] - my) * (p[1] - my); });
    return sxx > 0 && syy > 0 ? sxy / Math.sqrt(sxx * syy) : null;
  };
  var fair = function (d) { return 100 * (WX[d.f] || WX[d.w] || [1])[0]; };
  function add(area, name, what, applied, r) {
    if (!r || r.s == null || isNaN(r.s)) { missing.push(name); return; }   // a score that cannot be worked out is left out, never counted as 0
    r.s = Math.round(100 * cl(r.s)); r.area = area; r.name = name; r.what = what; r.applied = applied; out.push(r);
  }
  if (!O.length) return { list: [], missing: [] };
  var rev = sum(O, function (d) { return d.rev; }), last = days[days.length - 1], first = days[0];
  var startNW = first.nw - first.np, growth = last.nw - startNW, N = O.length;
  var cogsT = sum(O, function (d) { return d.cogs; }), wsT = sum(O, function (d) { return d.wst + d.sp; }), byT = sum(O, function (d) { return d.by; }), pbT = sum(O, function (d) { return d.pb; }), taT = sum(O, function (d) { return d.ta; });
  var fixedAvg = avg(O, function (d) { return d.fee + d.ad + (d.wg || 0) + (d.in || 0) + (d.lf || 0); }), of = function (x, n) { return x + ' of ' + n + ' days'; };

  /* --- Finance and accounting --- */
  var nm = rev > 0 ? k.profit / rev : null;
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Profitability (net profit margin)', 'Net profit margin = net profit ÷ sales. It shows how much of every rupee of sales the business keeps after all costs.',
    'Every day the P&L subtracts ingredients, waste, stand fee, advertising, wages, interest, fines and tax from sales.',
    nm == null ? null : { calc: 'Net margin = net profit ÷ sales = ' + M(k.profit) + ' ÷ ' + M(rev) + ' = ' + P(nm) + '. Profitable days: ' + of(days.filter(function (d) { return d.np > 0; }).length, days.length) + '.', s: (nm + 0.2) / 0.6, good: nm > 0.15 ? 'You kept ' + P(nm) + ' of sales as profit, and ' + P(k.profitableShare) + ' of days were profitable.' : '',
      bad: nm <= 0.15 ? (nm < 0 ? 'The business made a loss: net margin ' + P(nm) : 'Only ' + P(nm) + ' of sales became profit') + (k.profitableShare < 0.6 ? ', and just ' + P(k.profitableShare) + ' of days made money' : '') + '.' : '',
      tip: 'Watch the biggest cost line on each day\'s results screen and cut it first; price a little higher on hot days.' });
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Gross margin', 'Gross margin = (sales − cost of cups sold) ÷ sales: the money left to pay fixed costs and make profit.',
    'Cost of cups sold is lemons, sugar, ice and cups used. Recipe and price together set the gross margin.',
    k.grossMargin == null ? null : { calc: 'Gross margin = (sales − cost of cups sold) ÷ sales = (' + M(rev) + ' − ' + M(cogsT) + ') ÷ ' + M(rev) + ' = ' + P(k.grossMargin) + '.', s: k.grossMargin / 0.6, good: k.grossMargin >= 0.45 ? 'A healthy gross margin of ' + P(k.grossMargin) + '.' : '',
      bad: k.grossMargin < 0.45 ? 'Gross margin was only ' + P(k.grossMargin) + ': ingredients took too much of each sale.' : '',
      tip: 'Use the cheapest recipe that keeps taste above 80, and avoid pricing below the fair price for the weather.' });
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Contribution margin per cup', 'Contribution = price − variable cost per cup. Each cup sold contributes this amount towards fixed costs.',
    'Your average price was ' + M(k.avgPrice) + ' and each cup cost ' + M(k.costPerCup) + ' in ingredients.',
    k.contribution == null ? null : { calc: 'Contribution = average price − cost per cup = ' + M(k.avgPrice) + ' − ' + M(k.costPerCup) + ' = ' + M(k.contribution) + ' (' + P(k.contribution / k.avgPrice) + ' of price).', s: (k.contribution / k.avgPrice) / 0.7, good: k.contribution / k.avgPrice >= 0.5 ? 'Each cup contributed ' + M(k.contribution) + ' (' + P(k.contribution / k.avgPrice) + ' of its price).' : '',
      bad: k.contribution / k.avgPrice < 0.5 ? 'Each cup contributed only ' + M(k.contribution) + ', ' + P(k.contribution / k.avgPrice) + ' of its price.' : '',
      tip: 'Raise contribution by trimming lemons or sugar by one where taste stays high, or by raising price on busy days.' });
  var beHits = O.filter(function (d) { return d.sv > 0; }).map(function (d) {
    var c = d.sv ? (d.rev - d.cogs) / d.sv : 0, fx = d.fee + d.ad + (d.wg || 0) + (d.in || 0) + (d.lf || 0); return c > 0 ? d.sv >= fx / c : false; });
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Break-even analysis', 'Break-even volume = fixed costs ÷ contribution per cup: the cups you must sell before making any profit.',
    'Fixed costs each day are the stand fee, advertising, any helper wage and interest. The planning screen shows the break-even cups.',
    beHits.length ? { calc: 'Average fixed cost a day ' + M(fixedAvg) + ' ÷ contribution ' + M(k.contribution) + ' = ' + (k.contribution > 0 ? Math.ceil(fixedAvg / k.contribution) : '—') + ' cups to break even; you sold ' + Math.round(k.avgCups) + ' a day on average. Above break-even on ' + of(beHits.filter(function (x) { return x; }).length, beHits.length) + '.', s: share(beHits, function (x) { return x; }), good: share(beHits, function (x) { return x; }) >= 0.7 ? 'You sold above break-even on ' + P(share(beHits, function (x) { return x; })) + ' of open days.' : '',
      bad: share(beHits, function (x) { return x; }) < 0.7 ? 'You sold below break-even on ' + P(1 - share(beHits, function (x) { return x; })) + ' of open days.' : '',
      tip: 'Check the break-even number before opening; if the forecast crowd is smaller, cut advertising or the helper that day.' } : null);
  var lowCash = share(days, function (d) { return d.cash < 1000; });
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Working capital and liquidity', 'Working capital is the cash needed to run day to day. Running out of cash stops a business even when it is profitable.',
    'Cash pays for tomorrow\'s stock. The stand closes if cash cannot buy a full pitcher.',
    { calc: 'Days with cash below ' + M(1000) + ': ' + of(days.filter(function (d) { return d.cash < 1000; }).length, days.length) + ' = ' + P(lowCash) + '. Score = 100 − that share.', s: 1 - lowCash, good: lowCash < 0.2 ? 'You kept a cash cushion on ' + P(1 - lowCash) + ' of days (lowest cash ' + M(Math.min.apply(null, days.map(function (d) { return d.cash; }))) + ').' : '',
      bad: lowCash >= 0.2 ? 'Cash fell below ' + M(1000) + ' on ' + P(lowCash) + ' of days, leaving no buffer for bad weather.' : '',
      tip: 'Keep at least one day\'s stock money in reserve before buying upgrades or advertising.' });
  var borrowed = days.some(function (d) { return d.loan > 0; }), debtCost = k.interest + sum(days, function (d) { return d.lf || 0; }), intRatio = rev > 0 ? debtCost / rev : 0;
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Cost of debt and leverage', 'Leverage is using borrowed money. It helps only when the return beats the interest cost.',
    'Family loan fee, bank interest (2% a day) and the moneylender (20% a day) all appear as costs in the P&L.',
    !borrowed ? null : { calc: 'Borrowing cost ÷ sales = (interest ' + M(k.interest) + ' + loan fees ' + M(debtCost - k.interest) + ') ÷ ' + M(rev) + ' = ' + P(intRatio) + '. Days with a loan: ' + P(k.loanDays) + '.', s: 1 - intRatio / 0.3, good: intRatio < 0.1 ? 'Borrowing costs (interest and fees) took only ' + P(intRatio) + ' of sales (' + M(debtCost) + ').' : '',
      bad: intRatio >= 0.1 ? 'Borrowing costs (interest and fees) took ' + P(intRatio) + ' of sales (' + M(debtCost) + '); loans were on ' + P(k.loanDays) + ' of days.' : '',
      tip: 'Borrow only for something that raises profit by more than the interest, and repay the most expensive loan first.' });
  var raju = days.some(function (d) { return d.rj > 0; }), bank = days.some(function (d) { return d.bk > 0; });
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Choice of lender (cost of capital)', 'Different sources of money have different costs: family, bank, moneylender and equity investors.',
    'Family loans are cheap, the bank needs collateral and an EMI, and Raju charges 20% a day, compounding.',
    !borrowed ? null : { calc: 'Sources used: ' + [days.some(function (d) { return d.loan > (d.bk || 0) + (d.rj || 0); }) ? 'family' : '', bank ? 'bank (2% a day)' : '', raju ? 'moneylender (20% a day)' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', ') + '. Moneylender cost: ₹5,000 × 1.2⁵ = ₹12,442 after 5 days.', s: raju ? 0.3 : bank ? 0.8 : 0.9, good: !raju ? 'You avoided the moneylender and used cheaper money' + (bank ? ' from the bank.' : ' from family.') : '',
      bad: raju ? 'You borrowed from Raju at 20% a day: a ₹5,000 loan grows to about ₹12,400 in 5 days.' : '',
      tip: 'Compare the daily cost of each source on the Money screen before borrowing; never use the moneylender for routine stock.' });
  var seized = days.some(function (d) { return d.sz > 0; });
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Debt servicing and default risk', 'Lenders recover what they are owed from collateral; missing payments leads to seizure and, at worst, insolvency.',
    'Five missed bank EMIs and the bank sells your equipment; an unpaid moneylender breaks the stall.',
    !borrowed ? null : { calc: 'Equipment lost to lenders: ' + M(sum(days, function (d) { return d.sz || 0; })) + '.', s: seized ? 0 : 0.9, good: !seized ? 'You kept up with your loan payments and never lost equipment.' : '',
      bad: seized ? 'You lost equipment to a lender (' + M(sum(days, function (d) { return d.sz || 0; })) + ' written off).' : '',
      tip: 'Before borrowing, check that a normal day\'s profit covers the EMI at least 1.5 times (interest coverage).' });
  var g = growth / Math.max(Math.abs(startNW), 1000);
  add('Finance and accounting', 'Wealth creation (net worth and retained earnings)', 'Net worth = assets − liabilities. Growing it through retained profit is the real measure of a business.',
    'Statistics shows the balance sheet: cash, stock and equipment less all loans.',
    { calc: 'Net worth now ' + M(last.nw) + ' − at the start ' + M(startNW) + ' = ' + M(growth) + ' (' + P(g) + ' growth).', s: 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, g)), good: growth > 0 ? 'Net worth grew by ' + M(growth) + ' over the period.' : '',
      bad: growth <= 0 ? 'Net worth fell by ' + M(-growth) + ' over the period.' : '',
      tip: 'Judge each decision by its effect on net worth, not just on cash today.' });

  // Tax planning (new in grading v2): on each weekly tax day, was a day's stock money still left in cash after the tax?
  var taxD = days.filter(function (d) { return d.tx > 0; });
  if (taxD.length) {
    var need = avg(O, function (d) { return d.pur || 0; }) || 1, okTax = taxD.filter(function (d) { return d.cash >= need; }).length;
    add('Finance and accounting', 'Taxation and tax planning', 'Tax planning means setting aside cash for tax when it falls due, and treating tax as a normal cost of earning profit.',
      'Every seventh day 10% of the week\'s profit is taken as tax.',
      { calc: 'Tax days: ' + taxD.length + '; tax paid ' + M(sum(taxD, function (d) { return d.tx; })) + '. Days that still left a day\'s stock money (' + M(need) + ') in cash: ' + okTax + ' of ' + taxD.length + '.', s: okTax / taxD.length,
        good: okTax === taxD.length ? 'You always had stock money left after paying the weekly tax.' : '', bad: okTax < taxD.length ? 'On ' + (taxD.length - okTax) + ' tax days the tax left too little cash for the next day\'s stock.' : '',
        tip: 'Keep about 10% of each day\'s profit aside for the weekly tax.' });
  } else add('Finance and accounting', 'Taxation and tax planning', '', '', null);

  /* --- Operations and supply chain --- */
  var mt = O.filter(function (d) { return d.by > 0; }).map(function (d) { var made = d.sv + d.wp; return made && d.by ? Math.min(made, d.by) / Math.max(made, d.by) : 0; });
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Production planning (matching supply to demand)', 'Make enough to meet demand without overproducing. The gap in either direction costs money.',
    'Pitchers made each day set the cups available; buyers depend on weather, price and reputation.',
    mt.length ? { calc: 'For each day: smaller of (cups made, buyers) ÷ larger. Average over ' + of(mt.length, N) + ' = ' + P(avg(mt, function (x) { return x; })) + '. Over-made on ' + P(k.overMadeDays) + ' of days.', s: avg(mt, function (x) { return x; }), good: avg(mt, function (x) { return x; }) >= 0.75 ? 'Cups made matched buyers well (' + P(avg(mt, function (x) { return x; })) + ' match on average).' : '',
      bad: avg(mt, function (x) { return x; }) < 0.75 ? 'Cups made and buyers were often far apart (' + P(avg(mt, function (x) { return x; })) + ' match).' : '',
      tip: 'Use the forecast\'s expected buyers on the planning screen to decide pitchers, not yesterday\'s sales.' } : null);
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Inventory control and waste', 'Holding too much stock ties up cash and, for perishables, turns into waste.',
    'Unsold lemonade is poured away and old lemons spoil; both are shown as waste in the P&L.',
    k.waste == null ? null : { calc: 'Waste ÷ (ingredients used + waste) = ' + M(wsT) + ' ÷ ' + M(cogsT + wsT) + ' = ' + P(k.waste) + '.', s: 1 - k.waste / 0.3, good: k.waste < 0.1 ? 'Waste was low: ' + P(k.waste) + ' of ingredient cost.' : '',
      bad: k.waste >= 0.1 ? P(k.waste) + ' of ingredient cost was thrown away.' : '', tip: 'Buy lemons for the next two or three days only, and make one pitcher less on uncertain days.' });
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Capacity and stock-outs', 'A stock-out loses sales and customer goodwill; capacity limits how many customers you can serve.',
    'Customers are turned away when cups run out or the counter reaches its limit.',
    { calc: 'Days customers were turned away: ' + of(O.filter(function (d) { return d.ta > 0; }).length, N) + ' = ' + P(k.stockOutDays) + '. Buyers lost: ' + taT + ' of ' + byT + ' = ' + P(byT ? taT / byT : 0) + '.', s: 1 - k.stockOutDays, good: k.stockOutDays < 0.2 ? 'You rarely ran out (' + P(k.stockOutDays) + ' of days).' : '',
      bad: k.stockOutDays >= 0.2 ? 'You turned customers away on ' + P(k.stockOutDays) + ' of days (' + P(k.lostSales || 0) + ' of buyers lost).' : '',
      tip: 'On hot and sunny forecasts keep a little safety stock, or invest in a faster counter or helper.' });
  var spoil = sum(O, function (d) { return d.sp; }), ingred = sum(O, function (d) { return d.cogs + d.wst + d.sp; });
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Perishable goods (spoilage)', 'Perishable inventory loses value with time; first-in first-out and small orders reduce spoilage.',
    'Lemons spoil after a few days and ice melts overnight.',
    ingred > 0 ? { calc: 'Spoiled ÷ all ingredients bought = ' + M(spoil) + ' ÷ ' + M(ingred) + ' = ' + P(spoil / ingred) + '.', s: 1 - (spoil / ingred) / 0.15, good: spoil / ingred < 0.05 ? 'Almost nothing spoiled (' + P(spoil / ingred) + ').' : '',
      bad: spoil / ingred >= 0.05 ? P(spoil / ingred) + ' of ingredients spoiled before use (' + M(spoil) + ').' : '', tip: 'Buy ice daily and lemons in smaller lots; use older stock first.' } : null);
  var tsd = Math.sqrt(avg(O, function (d) { return (d.tas - k.taste) * (d.tas - k.taste); }));
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Product quality', 'Quality drives repeat customers and reputation; it must be managed, not left to chance.',
    'Taste (0–100) comes from the lemon and sugar balance and ice for the weather.',
    { calc: 'Average taste over ' + N + ' open days = ' + Math.round(k.taste) + ' out of 100.', s: k.taste / 100, good: k.taste >= 80 ? 'Average taste ' + Math.round(k.taste) + ': customers liked the product.' : '',
      bad: k.taste < 80 ? 'Average taste only ' + Math.round(k.taste) + ', which holds back reputation.' : '', tip: 'Test recipes on the Recipe tab until taste shows 80 or more, then keep that recipe.' });
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Process consistency', 'Consistent processes give predictable quality (standard operating procedures).',
    'How much your taste score varied from day to day.',
    O.length >= 3 ? { calc: 'Standard deviation of daily taste = ±' + Math.round(tsd) + ' points (score falls to 0 at ±20).', s: 1 - tsd / 20, good: tsd < 8 ? 'Quality was steady day to day (variation ±' + Math.round(tsd) + ').' : '',
      bad: tsd >= 8 ? 'Quality swung a lot from day to day (±' + Math.round(tsd) + ' points).' : '', tip: 'Fix a standard recipe and change only the ice with the weather.' } : null);
  var disc = O.filter(function (d) { return d.ev === 'discount'; }), other = O.filter(function (d) { return d.ev !== 'discount' && d.ev !== 'shortage'; });
  add('Operations and supply chain', 'Purchasing and supplier deals', 'Good buyers stock up when prices are low and buy only what they need when prices are high.',
    'Supplier discounts (25% off) and shortages (40% dearer) change the price of every pack.',
    disc.length && other.length ? (function () { var r = avg(disc, function (d) { return d.pur; }) / Math.max(1, avg(other, function (d) { return d.pur; }));
      return { calc: 'Average buying on discount days ' + M(avg(disc, function (d) { return d.pur; })) + ' ÷ on normal days ' + M(avg(other, function (d) { return d.pur; })) + ' = ' + r.toFixed(1) + '×.', s: r / 2, good: r >= 1.3 ? 'You bought ' + r.toFixed(1) + '× more than usual on discount days.' : '', bad: r < 1.3 ? 'You did not stock up on discount days (' + r.toFixed(1) + '× usual buying).' : '',
        tip: 'On a supplier discount, buy non-perishables (sugar, cups) for several days ahead.' }; })() : null);

  /* --- Marketing and pricing --- */
  var pr = avg(O, function (d) { return d.pr / fair(d); });
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Value-based pricing', 'Price should reflect what customers are willing to pay, not just cost.',
    'Customers judge your price against a fair price that rises on hot days and falls on cool and rainy days.',
    { calc: 'Average of (your price ÷ fair price for the forecast) = ' + P(pr) + '. Fair price = ' + M(100) + ' × weather factor (hot 1.2, sunny 1.0, cloudy 0.95, cool and rainy 0.85, stormy 0.8). Best near 105%.', s: 1 - Math.abs(pr - 1.05) / 0.5, good: Math.abs(pr - 1.05) <= 0.15 ? 'Your prices sat close to what customers expect (' + P(pr) + ' of the fair price).' : '',
      bad: Math.abs(pr - 1.05) > 0.15 ? 'Your average price was ' + P(pr) + ' of the fair price: ' + (pr > 1.05 ? 'too high, so fewer people bought.' : 'too low, leaving money on the table.') : '',
      tip: 'Start near the fair price shown in "What today looks like" and adjust in small steps.' });
  var pc = corr(O.map(function (d) { return [d.pr, fair(d)]; })), prices = Object.keys(O.reduce(function (a, d) { a[d.pr] = 1; return a; }, {})).length;
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Dynamic pricing (responding to demand)', 'Changing price with demand (weather, events) captures more value on busy days.',
    'Fair price changes with the weather; a good manager follows it.',
    { calc: (pc == null ? 'Distinct prices used: ' + prices + '.' : 'Correlation between your price and the fair price over ' + N + ' days = ' + pc.toFixed(2) + ' (1 = follows the weather exactly).'), s: pc == null ? (prices > 1 ? 0.4 : 0.2) : 0.5 + 0.5 * pc, good: pc != null && pc > 0.4 ? 'Your price rose and fell with the weather (correlation ' + pc.toFixed(2) + ').' : '',
      bad: pc == null || pc <= 0.4 ? (prices <= 1 ? 'You kept one price every day, whatever the weather.' : 'Your price changes did not follow the weather.') : '',
      tip: 'Raise price 10–20% on hot days and lower it on cool or rainy days.' });
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Price experimentation and elasticity', 'Price elasticity measures how buyers respond to price; you learn it by testing prices.',
    'Each price you try shows how many passers-by bought.',
    { calc: 'Prices tried: ' + prices + '. ' + (k.elasticity != null ? 'Elasticity = slope of ln(conversion) on ln(price) = ' + k.elasticity.toFixed(2) + '.' : 'Elasticity needs 5+ days with at least 2 different prices.'), s: k.elasticity != null ? 0.85 : Math.min(1, prices / 5), good: k.elasticity != null ? 'You tested enough prices to measure elasticity (' + k.elasticity.toFixed(2) + ': ' + (k.elasticity < -1 ? 'customers are price-sensitive' : 'customers are not very price-sensitive') + ').' : '',
      bad: k.elasticity == null ? 'You tried only ' + prices + ' price' + (prices === 1 ? '' : 's') + ', too few to learn how customers react.' : '', tip: 'Try at least three different prices on similar weather days and compare the conversion rate.' });
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Conversion rate', 'Conversion = buyers ÷ people reached. It reflects price, product and reputation together.',
    'Passers-by become buyers depending on price, taste and reputation.',
    k.conversion == null ? null : { calc: 'Buyers ÷ passers-by = ' + byT + ' ÷ ' + pbT + ' = ' + P(k.conversion) + '.', s: k.conversion / 0.5, good: k.conversion >= 0.35 ? P(k.conversion) + ' of passers-by bought: a strong offer.' : '',
      bad: k.conversion < 0.35 ? 'Only ' + P(k.conversion) + ' of passers-by bought.' : '', tip: 'Lift conversion with better taste first, then a fair price; advertising brings people but does not convince them.' });
  var adD = O.filter(function (d) { return d.ad > 0; });
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Marketing return on investment', 'Marketing ROI = extra profit from advertising ÷ advertising cost. Spend should be tested and measured.',
    'Advertising raises the number of passers-by at a daily cost.',
    { calc: (k.adReturn == null ? 'Advertising days: ' + adD.length + ' (needs 2+ days with and 2+ without ads).' : '(profit + ad cost on ad days ' + M(avg(adD, function (d) { return d.np + d.ad; })) + ' − profit on other days ' + M(avg(O.filter(function (d) { return !d.ad; }), function (d) { return d.np; })) + ') ÷ average ad spend ' + M(avg(adD, function (d) { return d.ad; })) + ' = ' + k.adReturn.toFixed(1) + '×.'), s: k.adReturn == null ? (adD.length ? 0.5 : 0.4) : k.adReturn, good: k.adReturn != null && k.adReturn >= 1 ? 'Advertising paid for itself (' + k.adReturn.toFixed(1) + '× return).' : '',
      bad: k.adReturn == null ? (adD.length ? 'You advertised too rarely to measure the return.' : 'You never tested advertising.') : k.adReturn < 1 ? 'Advertising returned only ' + k.adReturn.toFixed(1) + '× its cost.' : '',
      tip: 'Advertise on hot and sunny days when many people pass, and compare profit with similar days without ads.' });
  var goodWx = sum(adD.filter(function (d) { return d.f === 'hot' || d.f === 'sunny'; }), function (d) { return d.ad; }), allAd = sum(adD, function (d) { return d.ad; });
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Targeting marketing spend', 'Spend marketing where and when it reaches the most likely buyers.',
    'Ads work best when the forecast brings crowds (hot and sunny days).',
    allAd ? { calc: 'Ad spend on hot and sunny days ' + M(goodWx) + ' ÷ all ad spend ' + M(allAd) + ' = ' + P(goodWx / allAd) + '.', s: goodWx / allAd, good: goodWx / allAd >= 0.7 ? P(goodWx / allAd) + ' of ad spend went on hot and sunny days.' : '',
      bad: goodWx / allAd < 0.7 ? P(1 - goodWx / allAd) + ' of ad spend went on cloudy, cool or rainy days when few people pass.' : '', tip: 'Switch advertising off when the forecast is cool, rainy or stormy.' } : null);
  var pink = O.filter(function (d) { return d.pd && d.pd !== 'classic'; }), classic = O.filter(function (d) { return !d.pd || d.pd === 'classic'; });
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Product differentiation', 'Offering a distinct premium product lets you charge more to customers who value it.',
    'Pink lemonade is a premium variant with its own cost and price.',
    pink.length && classic.length ? (function () { var b = avg(pink, function (d) { return d.np; }) > avg(classic, function (d) { return d.np; });
      return { calc: 'Average daily profit: premium ' + M(avg(pink, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' (' + pink.length + ' days) vs classic ' + M(avg(classic, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' (' + classic.length + ' days).', s: b ? 0.9 : 0.5, good: b ? 'Your premium product earned more per day (' + M(avg(pink, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' vs ' + M(avg(classic, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ').' : '',
        bad: !b ? 'The premium product earned less than classic on your days (' + M(avg(pink, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' vs ' + M(avg(classic, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ').' : '', tip: 'Price a premium product clearly above classic, and sell it on busy days.' }; })() : null);
  var repCh = last.rep - (first.rep - (first.rd || 0));
  add('Marketing and pricing', 'Brand equity and reputation', 'Reputation is an asset built by consistent quality and fair prices; it brings future customers.',
    'Reputation (0–100) rises with happy customers and falls with poor taste, high prices or being closed.',
    { calc: 'Reputation now ' + last.rep + ' − at the start ' + (first.rep - (first.rd || 0)) + ' = ' + (repCh >= 0 ? '+' : '') + repCh + ' points.', s: 0.5 + repCh / 40, good: repCh > 0 ? 'Reputation rose by ' + repCh + ' points to ' + last.rep + '.' : '', bad: repCh <= 0 ? 'Reputation ' + (repCh < 0 ? 'fell by ' + (-repCh) : 'did not grow') + ' (now ' + last.rep + ').' : '',
      tip: 'Avoid turning customers away and keep taste high; reputation compounds over time.' });

  /* --- Economics and decision-making --- */
  var iceHit = share(O, function (d) { return d.ic === (WX[d.f] || [0, d.ic])[1]; });
  add('Economics and decision-making', 'Using forecasts (decisions under uncertainty)', 'Good managers act on the best available information, even when it is uncertain.',
    'The weather forecast tells you the ideal ice per cup and the likely crowd.',
    { calc: 'Days ice matched the forecast ideal: ' + of(O.filter(function (d) { return d.ic === (WX[d.f] || [0, d.ic])[1]; }).length, N) + ' = ' + P(iceHit) + '.', s: iceHit, good: iceHit >= 0.6 ? 'Ice matched the forecast on ' + P(iceHit) + ' of days.' : '', bad: iceHit < 0.6 ? 'Ice matched the forecast on only ' + P(iceHit) + ' of days.' : '',
      tip: 'Read the forecast first each day and set ice to the ideal shown (4 hot, 3 sunny, 2 cloudy, 1 otherwise).' });
  var stormF = days.filter(function (d) { return d.f === 'stormy'; });
  add('Economics and decision-making', 'Risk management', 'Reduce exposure when the downside is large: scale down, insure or stay out.',
    'Rainy and stormy days bring few customers; closing or making less limits losses.',
    k.badWeatherProfit == null && !stormF.length ? null : { calc: 'Average profit on rainy and stormy days: ' + (k.badWeatherProfit == null ? 'none open' : M(k.badWeatherProfit)) + '. Storm forecasts closed: ' + stormF.filter(function (d) { return d.cl; }).length + ' of ' + stormF.length + '.', s: (k.badWeatherProfit == null || k.badWeatherProfit >= 0 ? 0.6 : 0.2) + (stormF.length ? 0.4 * share(stormF, function (d) { return d.cl; }) : 0.3),
      good: k.badWeatherProfit != null && k.badWeatherProfit >= 0 ? 'You did not lose money on rainy or stormy days.' : stormF.length && share(stormF, function (d) { return d.cl; }) > 0.5 ? 'You closed on most storm forecasts.' : '',
      bad: k.badWeatherProfit != null && k.badWeatherProfit < 0 ? 'Rainy and stormy days lost ' + M(-k.badWeatherProfit) + ' each on average.' : '',
      tip: 'On rainy forecasts make one pitcher and skip ads; close for storms.' });
  add('Economics and decision-making', 'Loss aversion (behavioural bias)', 'People react more strongly to losses than gains, often cutting price in panic after a bad day.',
    'How often you cut the price the day after a loss.',
    k.cutAfterLoss == null ? null : { calc: 'Price cut the day after a loss: ' + P(k.cutAfterLoss) + ' of loss days (a balanced manager is near 40%).', s: 1 - Math.abs(k.cutAfterLoss - 0.4) / 0.6, good: k.cutAfterLoss <= 0.6 ? 'After losses you reacted calmly (price cut on ' + P(k.cutAfterLoss) + ' of them).' : '',
      bad: k.cutAfterLoss > 0.6 ? 'You cut the price after ' + P(k.cutAfterLoss) + ' of losing days.' : '', tip: 'After a loss, find the cause first (weather, waste, stock-out) before touching the price.' });
  add('Economics and decision-making', 'Learning curve', 'Performance should improve with experience as you learn from results.',
    'Average daily profit in your first week compared with your latest week.',
    k.first7 == null ? null : (function () { var gg = (k.last7 - k.first7) / Math.max(Math.abs(k.first7), 100);
      return { calc: 'Average daily profit, days 1–7: ' + M(k.first7) + '; latest 7 days: ' + M(k.last7) + '. Change = (' + M(k.last7) + ' − ' + M(k.first7) + ') ÷ ' + M(Math.max(Math.abs(k.first7), 100)) + ' = ' + P(gg) + '.', s: 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, gg)), good: gg > 0.1 ? 'Daily profit rose from ' + M(k.first7) + ' to ' + M(k.last7) + '.' : '', bad: gg <= 0.1 ? 'Daily profit went from ' + M(k.first7) + ' to ' + M(k.last7) + ', no clear improvement.' : '',
        tip: 'Change one thing at a time and read the insight on each results screen.' }; })());
  var comp = O.filter(function (d) { return d.ev === 'competitor'; }), nonComp = O.filter(function (d) { return d.ev !== 'competitor'; });
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Competitive response', 'When a rival enters, respond with price, quality or service to protect market share.',
    'A rival stand sells about 10% below the fair price and takes customers unless you respond.',
    comp.length && nonComp.length ? (function () { var r = avg(comp, function (d) { return d.pr / fair(d); }) / avg(nonComp, function (d) { return d.pr / fair(d); });
      return { calc: 'Your price ÷ fair price on rival days ' + P(avg(comp, function (d) { return d.pr / fair(d); })) + ' vs other days ' + P(avg(nonComp, function (d) { return d.pr / fair(d); })) + ' (ratio ' + r.toFixed(2) + ').', s: r <= 0.95 ? 0.9 : r <= 1.02 ? 0.6 : 0.3, good: r <= 0.95 ? 'You priced sharper when the rival appeared.' : '', bad: r > 0.95 ? 'You did not adjust your price when the rival appeared.' : '',
        tip: 'On rival days price at or just below the rival\'s price shown on the forecast card.' }; })() : null);

  // Responding to market events (new in grading v2): busy events need more stock (serve more, turn few away);
  // slow events need lower costs (no advertising, small or no loss).
  var BUSY = { festival: 1, sports: 1, holiday: 1 }, SLOW = { closure: 1, competitor: 1 };
  var evD = O.filter(function (d) { return BUSY[d.ev] || SLOW[d.ev]; }), plain = O.filter(function (d) { return !d.ev; });
  if (evD.length) {
    var base = function (d) { var same = plain.filter(function (x) { return x.w === d.w; }); return same.length ? avg(same, function (x) { return x.sv; }) : avg(plain, function (x) { return x.sv; }); };
    var evS = evD.map(function (d) {
      if (BUSY[d.ev]) { var b = base(d) || 1; return 0.6 * cl((d.sv / b - 1) / 0.3) + 0.4 * (d.by ? 1 - cl(d.ta / d.by / 0.2) : 1); }
      return (d.ad ? 0 : 0.5) + (d.np >= 0 ? 0.5 : 0.2);
    });
    var busyN = evD.filter(function (d) { return BUSY[d.ev]; }).length;
    add('Economics and decision-making', 'Responding to market events', 'Responding to market events means adjusting stock, price and spending when conditions change suddenly.',
      'Festivals, cricket matches and holidays bring more people; road closures and a rival\'s promotion bring fewer.',
      { calc: 'Event days: ' + evD.length + ' (' + busyN + ' busy, ' + (evD.length - busyN) + ' slow). Busy days: served more than a normal day of the same weather and turned few away; slow days: no advertising and no big loss. Average response score ' + P(avg(evS, function (x) { return x; })) + '.', s: avg(evS, function (x) { return x; }),
        good: avg(evS, function (x) { return x; }) >= 0.7 ? 'You read market events well and adjusted on the day.' : '', bad: avg(evS, function (x) { return x; }) < 0.5 ? 'You often ran event days like normal days.' : '',
        tip: 'On a festival or match day make more pitchers and price a little higher; on a closure day cut advertising and stock.' });
  } else add('Economics and decision-making', 'Responding to market events', '', '', null);

  /* --- Strategy and growth --- */
  var ups = Math.max.apply(null, days.map(function (d) { return d.up || 0; })), tier = Math.max.apply(null, days.map(function (d) { return d.tier || 1; }));
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Capital investment decisions', 'Investing in assets raises future capacity or quality; the payback period tells you if it is worth it.',
    'Upgrades cost money now and raise taste, capacity or traffic; each shows its payback days.',
    { calc: 'Upgrades owned at the end: ' + ups + '. Score = 40 + 20 per upgrade (20 with none).', s: ups ? Math.min(1, 0.4 + ups * 0.2) : 0.2, good: ups ? 'You invested in ' + ups + ' upgrade' + (ups > 1 ? 's' : '') + '.' : '', bad: !ups ? 'You made no investments in equipment.' : '',
      tip: 'Buy the upgrade with the shortest payback first, once you keep a cash reserve.' });
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Business growth and scale', 'Growing to a larger format raises fixed costs but also capacity and revenue (economies of scale).',
    'Moving up a stand tier needs enough profit and cash.',
    { calc: 'Highest stand tier reached: ' + tier + '; net worth change ' + M(growth) + '.', s: tier > 1 ? 1 : growth > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.2, good: tier > 1 ? 'You grew to tier ' + tier + '.' : '', bad: tier === 1 ? 'You stayed at the first tier.' : '', tip: 'Build cash and profit steadily; the Grow option appears when you qualify.' });
  var iv = sum(days, function (d) { return d.iv || 0; });
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Equity financing and dilution', 'Selling a share of the business brings money without repayment, but gives away part of every future profit.',
    'The angel investor offers money for 30% of profits; you can buy the stake back.',
    iv > 0 ? { calc: 'Investor\'s 30% share paid so far: ' + M(iv) + '.', s: 0.7, good: 'You raised equity instead of debt.', bad: 'The investor has taken ' + M(iv) + ' so far as their share.', tip: 'Buy the stake back once profits are steady; dilution is permanent otherwise.' } : null);
  // Timing of expansion: profit in the five days after moving up a tier vs the five days before
  var tu = -1; days.forEach(function (d, i) { if (tu < 0 && i > 0 && (d.tier || 1) > (days[i - 1].tier || 1)) tu = i; });
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Timing of expansion', 'Expand when demand and cash can carry the higher fixed costs; too early strains cash, too late leaves money on the table.',
    'Moving up a stand tier raises capacity and the daily fee.',
    tu > 0 ? (function () { var bf = avg(days.slice(Math.max(0, tu - 5), tu), function (d) { return d.np; }), af = days.slice(tu, tu + 5), aa = avg(af, function (d) { return d.np; });
      return { calc: 'Moved up on day ' + days[tu].d + '. Average profit 5 days before ' + M(bf) + ', after ' + M(aa) + ' (' + af.length + ' days).', s: aa >= bf ? 1 : aa > 0 ? 0.6 : 0.3,
        good: aa >= bf ? 'Profit rose after you expanded.' : '', bad: aa < bf ? 'Profit fell after you expanded (' + M(bf) + ' → ' + M(aa) + ').' : '', tip: 'Expand going into good weather, with cash left for a few slow days.' }; })() : null);
  // Return on investment: profit in the five days after each upgrade vs the five days before
  var ui = []; days.forEach(function (d, i) { if (i > 0 && (d.up || 0) > (days[i - 1].up || 0)) ui.push(i); });
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Return on investment (upgrades)', 'An investment is judged by what it adds to profit afterwards, not by what it costs.',
    'Each upgrade should lift profit (more taste, capacity or traffic) in the days after you buy it.',
    ui.length ? (function () { var up = ui.filter(function (i) { return avg(days.slice(i, i + 5), function (d) { return d.np; }) > avg(days.slice(Math.max(0, i - 5), i), function (d) { return d.np; }); }).length;
      return { calc: 'Upgrades bought: ' + ui.length + ' (days ' + ui.map(function (i) { return days[i].d; }).join(', ') + '); profit was higher in the 5 days after ' + up + ' of them.', s: 0.3 + 0.7 * up / ui.length,
        good: up ? up + ' of your ' + ui.length + ' upgrades were followed by higher profit.' : '', bad: up < ui.length ? (ui.length - up) + ' upgrade' + (ui.length - up > 1 ? 's were' : ' was') + ' followed by lower profit.' : '', tip: 'Buy the upgrade that fixes your biggest problem (stock-outs → counter, low taste → equipment).' }; })() : null);
  // Sustainable growth: share of 5-day windows in which net worth rose
  var win = []; for (var wi = 5; wi < days.length; wi++) win.push(days[wi].nw > days[wi - 5].nw);
  add('Strategy and growth', 'Sustainable growth', 'Lasting businesses grow steadily rather than in bursts followed by losses.',
    'Net worth should rise over most stretches of the game, not just overall.',
    win.length ? { calc: 'Net worth rose over ' + win.filter(Boolean).length + ' of ' + win.length + ' five-day stretches = ' + P(share(win, function (x) { return x; })) + '.', s: share(win, function (x) { return x; }),
      good: share(win, function (x) { return x; }) >= 0.7 ? 'Your net worth grew steadily.' : '', bad: share(win, function (x) { return x; }) < 0.7 ? 'Net worth fell over ' + win.filter(function (x) { return !x; }).length + ' of ' + win.length + ' stretches.' : '', tip: 'Protect the downside: smaller batches and lower costs on poor-weather days.' } : null);

  // Scaling through stand tiers (new in grading v2): moved up when demand outgrew the stand, and used the new capacity.
  var TIER_CAP = [60, 100, 150, 300, 500], tUp = days.findIndex(function (d, i) { return i > 0 && d.tier > days[i - 1].tier; });
  if (tUp > 0) {
    var aft = O.filter(function (d, i) { return d.d > days[tUp].d; }), cap = function (d) { return TIER_CAP[(d.tier || 1) - 1] || 60; };
    var use = aft.length ? avg(aft, function (d) { return Math.min(1, d.sv / cap(d)); }) : 0, bef = days.slice(Math.max(0, tUp - 10), tUp), pressure = bef.length ? share(bef, function (d) { return d.ta > 0; }) : 0;
    add('Strategy and growth', 'Scaling through stand tiers', 'Scaling through tiers means moving up to a bigger stand when demand outgrows the current one, and then using the new capacity.',
      'Each stand tier serves more cups a day but charges a higher daily fee.',
      { calc: 'Moved up to tier ' + days[tUp].tier + ' on day ' + days[tUp].d + '. Customers were turned away on ' + P(pressure) + ' of the 10 days before. Capacity used after moving: ' + P(use) + '.', s: 0.4 + 0.6 * cl(use / 0.7),
        good: use >= 0.7 ? 'You filled the bigger stand: the move paid off.' : '', bad: use < 0.5 ? 'After moving up you used only ' + P(use) + ' of the bigger stand\'s capacity, so the higher fee was partly wasted.' : '',
        tip: 'Move up when you turn customers away often, then use price and marketing to fill the extra capacity.' });
  } else if (N >= 15 && k.stockOutDays > 0.3) {
    add('Strategy and growth', 'Scaling through stand tiers', 'Scaling through tiers means moving up to a bigger stand when demand outgrows the current one.', 'Each stand tier serves more cups a day but charges a higher daily fee.',
      { calc: 'Never moved up, while customers were turned away on ' + P(k.stockOutDays) + ' of days.', s: 0.3, good: '', bad: 'Demand outgrew your stand but you did not move up.', tip: 'When you often turn customers away, check whether you qualify for the next stand.' });
  } else add('Strategy and growth', 'Scaling through stand tiers', '', '', null);

  /* --- People --- */
  var hd = O.filter(function (d) { return d.hp; }), nh = O.filter(function (d) { return !d.hp; });
  var baseCap = function (d) { return [60, 60, 100, 150, 300, 500][d.tier || 1]; };
  add('People and organisation', 'Workforce planning (staffing to demand)', 'Match staff to expected demand: extra hands on busy days, none on quiet ones.',
    'A helper adds 40 cups of capacity; without one, busy days turn buyers away.',
    (function () { var busy = function (d) { return d.f === 'hot' || d.f === 'sunny'; }, lost = nh.filter(function (d) { return d.ta > 0; }).length;
      if (hd.length) { var fit = hd.filter(busy).length; return { calc: 'Helper on ' + hd.length + ' days, ' + fit + ' of them hot or sunny forecasts; buyers turned away without a helper on ' + of(lost, nh.length) + '.', s: 0.3 + 0.7 * fit / hd.length - (nh.length ? 0.3 * lost / nh.length : 0),
        good: fit ? 'You used the helper on ' + fit + ' busy-forecast days.' : '', bad: fit < hd.length ? 'The helper worked ' + (hd.length - fit) + ' quiet-forecast days.' : '', tip: 'Hire for hot and sunny spells; let the helper go before cool or rainy days.' }; }
      return { calc: 'No helper hired; buyers turned away on ' + of(lost, N) + '.', s: 1 - 1.5 * lost / N, good: !lost ? 'Your capacity was enough without extra staff.' : '',
        bad: lost ? 'You turned buyers away on ' + lost + ' days without hiring help.' : '', tip: 'When you turn buyers away on busy days, a helper (₹300 a day) can pay for itself.' }; })());
  var streak = 0, unhappy = 0; days.forEach(function (d) { if (d.hp && streak >= 3) unhappy++; streak = d.np < 0 ? streak + 1 : 0; });
  add('People and organisation', 'Employee morale', 'Staff motivation falls after repeated failure; managers protect morale by avoiding long losing runs.',
    'After three loss-making days in a row the helper gets unhappy and taste drops by 5.',
    hd.length ? { calc: 'Helper days: ' + hd.length + '; days the helper worked after 3 or more losses in a row: ' + unhappy + '.', s: 1 - 2 * unhappy / hd.length,
      good: !unhappy ? 'You never let the helper work through a losing streak.' : '', bad: unhappy ? 'The helper worked ' + unhappy + ' day' + (unhappy > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' while unhappy after a losing streak.' : '', tip: 'Break losing runs fast: cut costs, fix the price, or let the helper go.' } : null);
  add('People and organisation', 'Labour productivity', 'Productivity is output per worker; extra staff only pay off if their capacity is used.',
    'The helper\'s 40 extra cups count only on days when you serve more than the stand alone could.',
    hd.length ? (function () { var used = avg(hd, function (d) { return cl((d.sv - baseCap(d)) / 40); });
      return { calc: 'Average use of the helper\'s 40 extra cups on helper days = ' + P(used) + ' (cups served above the stand\'s own capacity).', s: used,
        good: used >= 0.6 ? 'You made good use of the helper\'s extra capacity.' : '', bad: used < 0.6 ? 'Most of the helper\'s extra capacity went unused.' : '', tip: 'Keep the helper only when you expect to sell more than the stand can serve alone.' }; })() : null);
  add('People and organisation', 'Fixed costs and operating leverage (hiring)', 'A wage is a fixed cost; it pays off only when extra sales cover it (operating leverage).',
    'A helper costs a daily wage, adds capacity and gets unhappy after losing streaks.',
    hd.length ? (function () { var b = !nh.length || avg(hd, function (d) { return d.np; }) >= avg(nh, function (d) { return d.np; });
      return { calc: 'Average profit with the helper ' + M(avg(hd, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' (' + hd.length + ' days) vs without ' + (nh.length ? M(avg(nh, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' (' + nh.length + ' days)' : '—') + '; wage ' + M(600) + ' a day.', s: b ? 0.85 : 0.4, good: b ? 'Days with the helper earned ' + M(avg(hd, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' on average' + (nh.length ? ' vs ' + M(avg(nh, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' without.' : '.') : '',
        bad: !b ? 'Days with the helper earned less (' + M(avg(hd, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ' vs ' + M(avg(nh, function (d) { return d.np; })) + ').' : '', tip: 'Hire help only for periods of high demand; let go when crowds are small.' }; })() : null);

  /* --- Ethics and compliance --- */
  var visits = days.filter(function (d) { return d.of; }), bribes = visits.filter(function (d) { return d.of === 'bribe'; }).length, permit = days.some(function (d) { return d.pm; });
  add('Ethics and compliance', 'Regulatory compliance', 'Following the law (licences, permits) avoids fines, closures and reputational damage; compliance is an investment.',
    'Without a Street Vending Permit the municipal officer visits; the permit stops the visits for good.',
    visits.length || permit ? { calc: 'Officer visits: ' + visits.length + '; Street Vending Permit: ' + (permit ? 'yes' : 'no') + '.', s: permit ? 1 : 1 - (visits.length >= 3 ? 0.4 : 0.2), good: permit ? 'You bought the Street Vending Permit.' : '',
      bad: !permit ? 'The officer visited ' + visits.length + ' time' + (visits.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' and you never bought the permit.' : '', tip: 'Buy the permit early: it costs less than two bribes or one fine.' } : null);
  add('Ethics and compliance', 'Corruption as a recurring cost', 'A bribe is not a one-time fix: it creates an obligation that keeps coming back, and it is illegal.',
    'Bribing the officer brings him back every second day for more.',
    visits.length ? { calc: 'Bribes paid on ' + bribes + ' of ' + visits.length + ' visits = ' + P(bribes / visits.length) + '; total ' + M(sum(days, function (d) { return d.bb || 0; })) + '.', s: 1 - (bribes / visits.length) * 0.9, good: !bribes ? 'You never paid a bribe.' : '', bad: bribes ? 'You paid bribes on ' + bribes + ' of ' + visits.length + ' visits (' + M(sum(days, function (d) { return d.bb || 0; })) + ').' : '',
      tip: 'Choose the legal route: pay the fine once and buy the permit.' } : null);
  var paid = sum(days, function (d) { return (d.bb || 0) + (d.fi || 0); }), pIdx = -1; days.forEach(function (d, i) { if (pIdx < 0 && d.pm) pIdx = i; });
  var before = pIdx < 0 ? visits.length : days.slice(0, pIdx).filter(function (d) { return d.of; }).length;
  add('Ethics and compliance', 'Cost of compliance vs cost of non-compliance', 'Compare the one-time cost of doing it right with the repeated cost of fines and bribes.',
    'The permit costs ' + M(4000) + ' once; each fine or bribe costs money every visit.',
    visits.length || permit ? { calc: 'Paid in fines and bribes: ' + M(paid) + '; permit price ' + M(4000) + '; officer visits before the permit: ' + before + '.', s: permit ? 1 - 0.15 * before : paid > 4000 ? 0.15 : 0.45,
      good: permit ? 'You bought the permit' + (before ? ' after ' + before + ' visit' + (before > 1 ? 's' : '') : ' before any visit') + '.' : '', bad: !permit ? 'You spent ' + M(paid) + ' on fines and bribes instead of a ' + M(4000) + ' permit.' : before > 1 ? 'You waited ' + before + ' visits before buying the permit.' : '',
      tip: 'Buy the permit as soon as you can afford it: it pays for itself after about two visits.' } : null);
  ['Market segmentation', 'Seasonal demand', 'Franchise model', 'Accounts payable'].forEach(function (n) { missing.push(n + ' (not in the game yet)'); });
  return { list: out, missing: missing };
}
function conceptHtml(c) {
  var bar = '<div style="font-size:6pt;line-height:6pt">' + hbar(c.m, GCOL[c.gr.g], 50) + '</div>';
  return '<div style="border:1px solid #d1d5db;padding:6px 8px;margin:0 0 6px;page-break-inside:avoid"><table style="border:0"><tr><td style="border:0;padding:0"><b>' + e(c.name) + '</b></td><td style="border:0;padding:0;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap">Mark <b>' + c.m + '%</b> ' + gbadge(c.gr) + '</td></tr></table>' + bar +
    '<div class="muted" style="margin-top:4px"><b>Concept:</b> ' + e(c.what) + '<br><b>In the game:</b> ' + e(c.applied) + '</div>' +
    (c.calc ? '<div style="margin-top:3px;background:#f3f4f6;padding:3px 5px">🧮 <b>Calculation:</b> ' + e(c.calc) + ' Mark ' + c.m + '%, grade ' + c.gr.g + ' (' + c.gr.gp + ' points).</div>' : '') +
    (c.good ? '<div style="margin-top:3px">✅ <b>What went well:</b> ' + e(c.good) + '</div>' : '') + (c.bad ? '<div style="margin-top:3px">⚠️ <b>What went wrong:</b> ' + e(c.bad) + '</div>' : '') +
    '<div style="margin-top:3px">➡️ <b>How to improve:</b> ' + e(c.tip) + '</div></div>';
}

/* ---------- Scores: copied from the app (app/src/main/assets/www/js/journal.js). tests/run.js checks they match. ---------- */
var SK = (function () {
  function sum(a, f) { return a.reduce(function (s, x) { return s + f(x); }, 0); }
  function avg(a, f) { return a.length ? sum(a, f) / a.length : 0; }
  /* Least-squares slope of y on x. */
  function slope(pts) {
    if (pts.length < 2) return null;
    var mx = avg(pts, function (p) { return p[0]; }), my = avg(pts, function (p) { return p[1]; });
    var sxx = sum(pts, function (p) { return (p[0] - mx) * (p[0] - mx); });
    if (sxx < 1e-9) return null;
    return sum(pts, function (p) { return (p[0] - mx) * (p[1] - my); }) / sxx;
  }

  function kpis(days) {
    var open = days.filter(function (d) { return !d.cl; });
    var k = { days: days.length, open: open.length };
    if (!open.length) return k;
    k.profit = sum(days, function (d) { return d.np; });
    k.avgProfit = k.profit / days.length;
    k.profitableShare = days.filter(function (d) { return d.np > 0; }).length / days.length;
    var rev = sum(open, function (d) { return d.rev; }), cogs = sum(open, function (d) { return d.cogs; }), sv = sum(open, function (d) { return d.sv; });
    k.grossMargin = rev > 0 ? (rev - cogs) / rev : null;
    k.avgPrice = sv > 0 ? rev / sv : null;
    k.costPerCup = sv > 0 ? cogs / sv : null;
    k.contribution = k.avgPrice != null ? k.avgPrice - k.costPerCup : null;
    var fixed = avg(open, function (d) { return d.fee + d.ad; });
    k.breakEven = k.contribution > 0 ? Math.ceil(fixed / k.contribution) : null;
    k.avgCups = sv / open.length;
    var pb = sum(open, function (d) { return d.pb; }), by = sum(open, function (d) { return d.by; }), ta = sum(open, function (d) { return d.ta; });
    k.conversion = pb > 0 ? by / pb : null;
    k.lostSales = by > 0 ? ta / by : null;
    var wst = sum(open, function (d) { return d.wst + d.sp; });
    k.waste = cogs + wst > 0 ? wst / (cogs + wst) : null;
    k.stockOutDays = open.filter(function (d) { return d.ta > 0; }).length / open.length;
    k.overMadeDays = open.filter(function (d) { return d.wp > 0; }).length / open.length;
    // price elasticity: slope of ln(conversion) on ln(price), using days with buyers
    var pts = open.filter(function (d) { return d.pb > 0 && d.by > 0 && d.pr > 0; }).map(function (d) { return [Math.log(d.pr), Math.log(d.by / d.pb)]; });
    var prices = {}; open.forEach(function (d) { prices[d.pr] = 1; });
    k.elasticity = pts.length >= 5 && Object.keys(prices).length >= 2 ? slope(pts) : null;
    // marketing return: extra profit on advertising days per unit of ad spend
    var adD = open.filter(function (d) { return d.ad > 0; }), noAd = open.filter(function (d) { return d.ad === 0; });
    k.adDays = adD.length;
    k.adReturn = adD.length >= 2 && noAd.length >= 2 ? (avg(adD, function (d) { return d.np + d.ad; }) - avg(noAd, function (d) { return d.np; })) / avg(adD, function (d) { return d.ad; }) : null;
    // risk and behaviour
    k.loanDays = days.filter(function (d) { return d.loan > 0; }).length / days.length;
    var afterLoss = 0, cutAfterLoss = 0;
