    for (var i = 1; i < days.length; i++) if (days[i - 1].np < 0 && days[i].g === days[i - 1].g && !days[i].cl) { afterLoss++; if (days[i].pr < days[i - 1].pr) cutAfterLoss++; }
    k.cutAfterLoss = afterLoss >= 3 ? cutAfterLoss / afterLoss : null;
    var storm = open.filter(function (d) { return d.w === 'stormy' || d.w === 'rainy'; });
    k.badWeatherProfit = storm.length ? avg(storm, function (d) { return d.np; }) : null;
    // learning curve: first 7 vs latest 7 days
    if (days.length >= 14) { k.first7 = avg(days.slice(0, 7), function (d) { return d.np; }); k.last7 = avg(days.slice(-7), function (d) { return d.np; }); }
    k.taste = avg(open, function (d) { return d.tas; });
    // compliance: the officer's visits and how they were handled
    k.visits = days.filter(function (d) { return d.of; }).length;
    k.bribes = days.filter(function (d) { return d.of === 'bribe'; }).length;
    k.permit = days.some(function (d) { return d.pm; });
    k.interest = sum(days, function (d) { return d.in || 0; });
    return k;
  }

  /* ---------- MBA skill scores (0–100) with one feedback line per area ----------
   * Copied into backend/archive/Reports.gs for the teacher's PDF reports: keep the two in step. */
  var MIN_SKILL_DAYS = 5;
  function skills(k) {
    if (!k.open || k.days < MIN_SKILL_DAYS) return null;
    var cl = function (x) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x)); }, P = function (x) { return Math.round(x * 100) + '%'; };
    var areas = [];
    var gm = k.grossMargin == null ? 0 : k.grossMargin;
    areas.push({ id: 'fin', name: 'Finance and accounting', concepts: 'Profitability, gross margin, working capital, leverage',
      score: 50 * k.profitableShare + 25 * (1 - k.loanDays) + 25 * cl(gm / 0.6),
      tip: k.loanDays > 0.3 ? 'Relied on loans on ' + P(k.loanDays) + ' of days. Plan cash for tomorrow\'s stock before spending (working capital).'
        : k.profitableShare < 0.6 ? 'Only ' + P(k.profitableShare) + ' of days were profitable. Compare price with cost per cup every day (contribution margin).'
        : gm < 0.4 ? 'Gross margin is thin (' + P(gm) + '). Cut ingredient cost or raise price where customers allow it.'
        : 'Strong: profitable on ' + P(k.profitableShare) + ' of days with a ' + P(gm) + ' gross margin and little borrowing.' });
    var waste = k.waste == null ? 0 : k.waste;
    areas.push({ id: 'ops', name: 'Operations and supply chain', concepts: 'Inventory, perishables, capacity, matching supply to demand',
      score: 50 * (1 - cl(waste / 0.3)) + 25 * (1 - k.stockOutDays) + 25 * (1 - k.overMadeDays),
      tip: waste > 0.15 ? P(waste) + ' of ingredient cost was wasted. Buy closer to expected demand (inventory control of perishables).'
        : k.stockOutDays > 0.3 ? 'Ran out of stock on ' + P(k.stockOutDays) + ' of days and lost sales. Keep a little safety stock on busy days.'
        : k.overMadeDays > 0.5 ? 'Poured lemonade away on ' + P(k.overMadeDays) + ' of days. Match production to the forecast.'
        : 'Strong: low waste (' + P(waste) + ') and few stock-outs. Supply matched demand well.' });
    var conv = k.conversion == null ? 0 : k.conversion;
    areas.push({ id: 'mkt', name: 'Marketing and pricing', concepts: 'Price elasticity, product quality, conversion, marketing return',
      score: 40 * cl(conv / 0.5) + 30 * k.taste / 100 + 30 * (k.adReturn == null ? 0.5 : cl(k.adReturn)),
      tip: k.taste < 75 ? 'Average taste was ' + Math.round(k.taste) + '. Balance lemons and sugar and match ice to the weather (product quality).'
        : conv < 0.3 ? 'Only ' + P(conv) + ' of passers-by bought. The price may be above what customers expect (price elasticity).'
        : k.adReturn != null && k.adReturn < 1 ? 'Advertising did not pay for itself. Measure the return before spending more (marketing ROI).'
        : !k.adDays ? 'Never tried advertising. Test it on busy days and compare the results.'
        : 'Strong: good taste, ' + P(conv) + ' of passers-by bought, and advertising paid back.' });
    var bw = k.badWeatherProfit, cut = k.cutAfterLoss;
    areas.push({ id: 'risk', name: 'Risk and decision-making', concepts: 'Forecasting, risk management, loss aversion, test and learn',
      score: 40 * (bw == null ? 0.5 : bw >= 0 ? 1 : 0.3) + 30 * (cut == null ? 0.5 : cl(1 - Math.abs(cut - 0.4) / 0.6)) + 30 * (k.elasticity != null ? 1 : 0.3),
      tip: bw != null && bw < 0 ? 'Lost money on rainy and stormy days. Scale down or close when the forecast is bad (risk management).'
        : cut != null && cut > 0.7 ? 'Cut the price after ' + P(cut) + ' of loss days. Find the cause before reacting (loss aversion).'
        : k.elasticity == null ? 'Tried too few prices to learn how customers respond. Experiment with price (test and learn).'
        : 'Strong: handled bad weather and reacted to losses calmly.' });
    if (k.visits || k.permit) {
      var bs = k.visits ? k.bribes / k.visits : 0;
      areas.push({ id: 'eth', name: 'Ethics and compliance', concepts: 'Regulatory compliance, corruption as a recurring cost, governance',
        score: 100 - 80 * bs - (!k.permit && k.visits >= 3 ? 20 : 0),
        tip: k.bribes ? 'Paid bribes on ' + k.bribes + ' of ' + k.visits + ' officer visits. A bribe comes back every second day; the permit is a one-time cost (compliance as investment).'
          : !k.permit && k.visits >= 3 ? 'Paid fines or closed on ' + k.visits + ' officer visits. A Street Vending Permit would have stopped them.'
          : 'Strong: stayed within the rules' + (k.permit ? ' and bought the Street Vending Permit.' : '.') });
    }
    if (k.first7 != null) {
      var gain = (k.last7 - k.first7) / Math.max(Math.abs(k.first7), 100);
      areas.push({ id: 'learn', name: 'Learning and growth', concepts: 'Learning curve, continuous improvement',
        score: 50 + 50 * Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, gain)),
        tip: gain > 0.1 ? 'Daily profit rose from the first week to the latest. Clear learning.' : gain < -0.1 ? 'Daily profit fell from the first week to the latest. Review what changed.' : 'Daily profit stayed about the same. Try one new idea at a time.' });
    }
    var tot = 0;
    areas.forEach(function (a) { a.score = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(100, a.score))); tot += a.score; });
    var overall = Math.round(tot / areas.length);
    return { overall: overall, grade: overall >= 80 ? 'Excellent' : overall >= 65 ? 'Good' : overall >= 50 ? 'Fair' : 'Needs work', areas: areas };
  }

  return { kpis: kpis, skills: skills };
})();

"d": "Value-based pricing sets price by what customers are willing to pay, rather than by cost alone.",
"n": "Customers pay for value, not for your costs. A bottle of water costs more at a cricket stadium than in a shop because its value there is higher. Price close to the customer's idea of a fair price for the situation: too high loses buyers, too low leaves money on the table.",
"f": "Price index = Your price ÷ Fair price for the conditions × 100",
"full": "Price about 105% of the fair price"
},
"Dynamic pricing (responding to demand)": {
"d": "Dynamic pricing changes price with demand conditions such as weather, time or events.",
"n": "Ola and Uber raise fares when demand peaks; hotels charge more in wedding season. In a lemonade stand, hot days bring thirsty customers who accept higher prices, while cool days need lower prices. Following demand captures more value on busy days and keeps sales going on slow ones.",
"f": "Responsiveness = Correlation of your price with the fair price over time",
"full": "Price follows demand exactly (correlation 1)"

"Price experimentation and elasticity": {
"d": "Price elasticity of demand measures how much the quantity bought changes when the price changes.",
"n": "Managers learn elasticity by testing prices. If a 10% price rise reduces buyers by 20%, demand is elastic (elasticity −2) and the rise loses revenue; if buyers fall only 5%, it gains. Testing a few prices in similar conditions shows which side of that line your product is on.",
"f": "Elasticity = % change in quantity ÷ % change in price",
"full": "5 or more prices tried and elasticity learned"

"f": "Conversion = Buyers ÷ People reached × 100",
"full": "Half of passers-by buy (50%)"

"f": "Marketing ROI = (Extra profit from ads − Ad cost) ÷ Ad cost",

"full": "Right ice every day"

"Risk management": {

"n": "A wedding caterer who keeps a backup generator manages risk. In the game, storms bring few customers but the same fixed costs. Closing or scaling down on a storm forecast cuts the loss. Managers compare the possible loss with the possible gain before committing.",
"f": "Downside = Average profit on bad-weather days",
"full": "No losses in bad weather"

"Loss aversion (behavioural bias)": {
"d": "Loss aversion is the tendency to feel losses more strongly than equal gains, which leads to panicky decisions after a loss.",
"n": "Behavioural economists (Kahneman and Tversky) showed people weigh a ₹100 loss about twice as heavily as a ₹100 gain. After a bad day, a shopkeeper may slash prices out of fear, even when the loss came from weather, not price. Good managers find the cause before reacting.",
"f": "Reaction rate = Days price was cut after a loss ÷ Loss days",
"full": "Cuts after about 40% of loss days, not in panic"

var WXN = { hot: 'Hot', sunny: 'Sunny', cloudy: 'Cloudy', cool: 'Cool', rainy: 'Rainy', stormy: 'Stormy' }, WXO = ['hot', 'sunny', 'cloudy', 'cool', 'rainy', 'stormy'];
var WXC = { hot: '#f59e0b', sunny: '#fbbf24', cloudy: '#9ca3af', cool: '#a5b4fc', rainy: '#60a5fa', stormy: '#1e3a8a' };
var EVN = { festival: 'Festival', sports: 'Cricket match', holiday: 'School holiday', closure: 'Road closure', competitor: 'Rival promotion', discount: 'Supplier discount', shortage: 'Supplier shortage' };

/* ---------- small SVG helpers ---------- */
function sv(w, h, body, label) { return '<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 ' + w + ' ' + h + '" width="100%" role="img" aria-label="' + e(label) + '" style="font-family:inherit;font-size:11px">' + body + '</svg>'; }
function tx(x, y, s, o) { o = o || {}; return '<text x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + y.toFixed(1) + '" font-size="' + (o.size || 11) + '" fill="' + (o.fill || '#111827') + '" text-anchor="' + (o.a || 'start') + '" font-weight="' + (o.w || 400) + '">' + e(s) + '</text>'; }
function svBars(rows, maxv) {   // rows: [label, value, ticks[], colour]
  maxv = maxv || 100; var W = 680, rh = 24, H = rows.length * rh + 12, lab = 230, bw = W - lab - 56, b = '';
  rows.forEach(function (r, i) {
    var y = 6 + i * rh, ln = clamp01(r[1] / maxv) * bw;
    b += tx(lab - 8, y + 14, r[0], { a: 'end', size: 11 }) + '<rect x="' + lab + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" width="' + bw + '" height="13" rx="3" fill="#e5e7eb"/>' +
      '<rect x="' + lab + '" y="' + (y + 4) + '" width="' + ln.toFixed(1) + '" height="13" rx="3" fill="' + (r[3] || '#2563eb') + '"/>' + tx(lab + bw + 6, y + 15, Math.round(r[1]), { size: 11 });
    (r[2] || []).forEach(function (t, j) { if (t == null) return; var x = lab + clamp01(t / maxv) * bw; b += '<line x1="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" x2="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y1="' + (y + 1) + '" y2="' + (y + 20) + '" stroke="' + (j ? '#16a34a' : '#111827') + '" stroke-width="2"/>'; });
  });
  return sv(W, H, b, 'bars');
}
function svLine(pts, o) {   // pts: [x, y]; o: {color, marks:[[x,label]], fmt}
  o = o || {}; var W = 680, H = o.h || 210, L = 74, R = 16, T = 14, B = 28, fmt = o.fmt || function (v) { return Math.round(v); };
  var xs = pts.map(function (p) { return p[0]; }), ys = pts.map(function (p) { return p[1]; }), x0 = Math.min.apply(null, xs), x1 = Math.max.apply(null, xs);
  var y0 = Math.min(0, Math.min.apply(null, ys)), y1 = Math.max.apply(null, ys); if (y1 === y0) y1 = y0 + 1; if (x1 === x0) x1 = x0 + 1;
  var X = function (x) { return L + (x - x0) / (x1 - x0) * (W - L - R); }, Y = function (y) { return T + (1 - (y - y0) / (y1 - y0)) * (H - T - B); }, b = '';
  for (var k = 0; k <= 4; k++) { var v = y0 + (y1 - y0) * k / 4; b += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + Y(v).toFixed(1) + '" y2="' + Y(v).toFixed(1) + '" stroke="#e5e7eb"/>' + tx(L - 6, Y(v) + 4, fmt(v), { a: 'end', size: 10, fill: '#6b7280' }); }
  b += '<polyline fill="none" stroke="' + (o.color || '#2563eb') + '" stroke-width="1.6" points="' + pts.map(function (p) { return X(p[0]).toFixed(1) + ',' + Y(p[1]).toFixed(1); }).join(' ') + '"/>';
  for (k = 0; k <= 5; k++) { var xv = Math.round(x0 + (x1 - x0) * k / 5); b += tx(X(xv), H - 9, 'Day ' + xv, { a: 'middle', size: 10, fill: '#6b7280' }); }
  (o.marks || []).forEach(function (m, i) { var xx = X(m[0]), right = xx > W - 170; b += '<line x1="' + xx.toFixed(1) + '" x2="' + xx.toFixed(1) + '" y1="' + T + '" y2="' + (H - B) + '" stroke="#6b7280" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/>' + tx(right ? xx - 3 : xx + 3, T + 10 + (i % 4) * 12, m[1], { size: 9.5, a: right ? 'end' : 'start' }); });
  return sv(W, H, b, o.label || 'line');
}
function svProfit(days, cur) {
  var W = 680, H = 190, L = 74, R = 16, T = 10, B = 26, v = days.map(function (d) { return d.np; }), hi = Math.max.apply(null, v.concat([1])), lo = Math.min.apply(null, v.concat([0]));
  var n = days.length, bw = (W - L - R) / n, Y = function (y) { return T + (1 - (y - lo) / (hi - lo)) * (H - T - B); }, b = '';
  for (var k = 0; k <= 4; k++) { var vv = lo + (hi - lo) * k / 4; b += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + Y(vv).toFixed(1) + '" y2="' + Y(vv).toFixed(1) + '" stroke="#e5e7eb"/>' + tx(L - 6, Y(vv) + 4, money(vv, cur), { a: 'end', size: 10, fill: '#6b7280' }); }
  days.forEach(function (d, i) { var y0 = Y(0), y1 = Y(d.np); b += '<rect x="' + (L + i * bw).toFixed(2) + '" y="' + Math.min(y0, y1).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + Math.max(bw, 0.6).toFixed(2) + '" height="' + Math.abs(y1 - y0).toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + (d.cl ? '#6b7280' : WXC[d.w] || '#9ca3af') + '"/>'; });
  b += '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + Y(0).toFixed(1) + '" y2="' + Y(0).toFixed(1) + '" stroke="#111827"/>';
  for (k = 0; k <= 5; k++) { var i = Math.round((n - 1) * k / 5); b += tx(L + i * bw, H - 8, 'Day ' + days[i].d, { a: 'middle', size: 10, fill: '#6b7280' }); }
  return sv(W, H, b, 'Daily profit by weather');
}
function svCols(cats, series, names, colors, fmt) {
  var W = 680, H = 210, L = 46, R = 16, T = 24, B = 30, hi = Math.max.apply(null, [].concat.apply([1], series)), lo = Math.min.apply(null, [].concat.apply([0], series));
  var band = (W - L - R) / cats.length, k = series.length, bw = Math.min(28, band / (k + 1)), Y = function (y) { return T + (1 - (y - lo) / (hi - lo)) * (H - T - B); }, b = '';
  names.forEach(function (nm, j) { b += '<rect x="' + (L + j * 130) + '" y="4" width="10" height="10" fill="' + colors[j] + '"/>' + tx(L + 14 + j * 130, 13, nm); });
  cats.forEach(function (c, i) {
    series.forEach(function (s, j) { var x = L + i * band + band / 2 - k * bw / 2 + j * bw, y1 = Y(s[i]), y0 = Y(0);
      b += '<rect x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + Math.min(y0, y1).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + (bw - 3).toFixed(1) + '" height="' + Math.abs(y1 - y0).toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + colors[j] + '"/>' + tx(x + (bw - 3) / 2, Math.min(y0, y1) - 4, fmt(s[i]), { a: 'middle', size: 9.5 }); });
    b += tx(L + i * band + band / 2, H - 12, c, { a: 'middle', size: 10.5 });
  });
  return sv(W, H, b + '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - R) + '" y1="' + Y(0).toFixed(1) + '" y2="' + Y(0).toFixed(1) + '" stroke="#111827"/>', 'columns');
}
function svBridge(steps) {   // [label, value, 'add'|'sub'|'total']
  var W = 680, H = 220, L = 26, T = 18, B = 36, n = steps.length, band = (W - L - 16) / n, bw = band * 0.6, run = 0, Y = function (y) { return T + (1 - y / 100) * (H - T - B); }, b = '';
  steps.forEach(function (st, i) {
    var x = L + i * band + (band - bw) / 2, a, z, c;
    if (st[2] === 'total') { a = 0; z = st[1]; c = i === n - 1 ? '#2563eb' : '#9ca3af'; run = st[1]; }
    else if (st[2] === 'add') { a = run; z = run + st[1]; c = '#16a34a'; run = z; } else { a = run - st[1]; z = run; c = '#dc2626'; run = a; }
    b += '<rect x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="' + Y(Math.max(a, z)).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + bw.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + Math.abs(Y(a) - Y(z)).toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + c + '"/>' +
      tx(x + bw / 2, Y(Math.max(a, z)) - 5, (st[2] === 'sub' ? '−' : st[2] === 'add' ? '+' : '') + st[1], { a: 'middle', w: 600 }) + tx(x + bw / 2, H - 20, st[0], { a: 'middle', size: 10.5 });
  });
  return sv(W, H, b + '<line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - 16) + '" y1="' + Y(0) + '" y2="' + Y(0) + '" stroke="#111827"/><line x1="' + L + '" x2="' + (W - 16) + '" y1="' + Y(40) + '" y2="' + Y(40) + '" stroke="#6b7280" stroke-dasharray="4 3"/>' + tx(W - 18, Y(40) - 4, 'Pass mark 40', { a: 'end', size: 10, fill: '#6b7280' }), 'Marks bridge');
}
function svStack(parts, cur) {   // [label, value, colour]
  var W = 680, tot = parts.reduce(function (t, p) { return t + Math.max(0, p[1]); }, 0) || 1, x = 0, b = '', rows = Math.ceil(parts.length / 4);
  parts.forEach(function (p) { var w = Math.max(0, p[1]) / tot * (W - 10); b += '<rect x="' + x.toFixed(1) + '" y="6" width="' + w.toFixed(1) + '" height="24" fill="' + p[2] + '"/>' + (w > 44 ? tx(x + w / 2, 22, Math.round(100 * p[1] / tot) + '%', { a: 'middle', fill: '#fff', w: 600, size: 10.5 }) : ''); x += w; });
  parts.forEach(function (p, i) { var lx = (i % 4) * 170, ly = 48 + Math.floor(i / 4) * 16; b += '<rect x="' + lx + '" y="' + (ly - 9) + '" width="10" height="10" fill="' + p[2] + '"/>' + tx(lx + 14, ly, p[0] + ' ' + money(p[1], cur), { size: 10 }); });
  return sv(W, 40 + rows * 16 + 8, b, 'Where each rupee of sales went');
}

/* ---------- facts about one attempt ---------- */
function attemptFacts(days) {
  var sum = function (f) { return days.reduce(function (t, d) { return t + (f(d) || 0); }, 0); }, n = days.length;
  var pl = { rev: sum(function (d) { return d.rev; }), cogs: sum(function (d) { return d.cogs; }), wst: sum(function (d) { return d.wst; }), sp: sum(function (d) { return d.sp; }),
    fee: sum(function (d) { return d.fee; }), ad: sum(function (d) { return d.ad; }), wg: sum(function (d) { return d.wg; }), int: sum(function (d) { return (d.in || 0) + (d.lf || 0); }),
    fine: sum(function (d) { return (d.fi || 0) + (d.pn || 0); }), bribe: sum(function (d) { return d.bb; }), tax: sum(function (d) { return d.tx; }), inv: sum(function (d) { return d.iv; }), np: sum(function (d) { return d.np; }) };
  pl.other = pl.np - (pl.rev - pl.cogs - pl.wst - pl.sp - pl.fee - pl.ad - pl.wg - pl.int - pl.fine - pl.bribe - pl.tax - pl.inv);
  var W = {}; days.forEach(function (d) { var w = d.cl ? 'closed' : d.w, o = W[w] = W[w] || { days: 0, pr: 0, sv: 0, by: 0, pb: 0, np: 0, ad: 0, ta: 0 };
    o.days++; o.pr += d.pr; o.sv += d.sv; o.by += d.by; o.pb += d.pb; o.np += d.np; o.ad += d.ad || 0; o.ta += d.ta; });
  var blk = Math.max(5, Math.ceil(n / 12 / 5) * 5), chunks = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i += blk) { var c = days.slice(i, i + blk), cs = function (f) { return c.reduce(function (t, d) { return t + (f(d) || 0); }, 0); };
    chunks.push({ from: c[0].d, to: c[c.length - 1].d, np: cs(function (d) { return d.np; }) / c.length, pr: cs(function (d) { return d.pr; }) / c.length, sv: cs(function (d) { return d.sv; }) / c.length,
      waste: cs(function (d) { return d.wst + d.sp; }) / Math.max(1, cs(function (d) { return d.cogs + d.wst + d.sp; })), taste: cs(function (d) { return d.tas; }) / c.length,
      loss: c.filter(function (d) { return d.np < 0; }).length, ta: c.filter(function (d) { return d.ta > 0; }).length }); }
  var ev = []; days.forEach(function (d, i) { var p = i ? days[i - 1] : null;
    if (d.of) ev.push([d.d, d.of === 'bribe' ? 'Officer visit: paid a bribe' : d.of === 'refuse' ? 'Officer visit: refused (penalty)' : 'Officer visit: ' + d.of]);
    if (p && d.up > p.up) ev.push([d.d, 'Bought an upgrade (now ' + d.up + ')']); if (p && d.up < p.up) ev.push([d.d, 'Sold an upgrade']);
    if (p && d.tier > p.tier) ev.push([d.d, 'Moved up to stand tier ' + d.tier]); if (d.pm && !(p && p.pm)) ev.push([d.d, 'Bought the Street Vending Permit']);
    if (d.hp && !(p && p.hp) && !ev.some(function (x) { return /helper/.test(x[1]); })) ev.push([d.d, 'First hired a helper']);
    if (d.rj > 0 && !(p && p.rj)) ev.push([d.d, 'Borrowed from the moneylender']); if (d.bk > 0 && !(p && p.bk)) ev.push([d.d, 'Took a bank loan']);
    if (d.sz > 0) ev.push([d.d, 'Equipment seized by a lender']); if (d.iv && !ev.some(function (x) { return /investor/.test(x[1]); })) ev.push([d.d, 'Angel investor starts taking a share of profit']);
    if (p && d.pd !== p.pd) ev.push([d.d, 'Switched to ' + (d.pd === 'pink' ? 'pink lemonade' : 'classic lemonade')]); });
  var best = days.reduce(function (a, b) { return b.np > a.np ? b : a; }, days[0]), worst = days.reduce(function (a, b) { return b.np < a.np ? b : a; }, days[0]);
  return { n: n, pl: pl, W: W, chunks: chunks, ev: ev, best: best, worst: worst, fixed: pl.fee / n + pl.ad / n + pl.wg / n + pl.int / n };
}
function pctile(v, p) { v = v.filter(function (x) { return x != null && !isNaN(x); }).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }); return v.length ? v[Math.floor(p * (v.length - 1))] : null; }

/* ---------- self-check exercises: question and answer, using the student's own numbers where they help ---------- */
function exercise(name, s, F) {
  var k = s.k, cur = s.cur, m = function (x) { return money(x, cur); }, n = s.days.length, pl = F.pl;
  var X = {
    'Profitability (net profit margin)': ['Your sales were ' + m(pl.rev) + ' and net profit ' + m(pl.np) + '. If advertising (' + m(pl.ad) + ') had been cut to zero with no loss of sales, what would your net margin have been?', pc(pl.rev ? (pl.np + pl.ad) / pl.rev : 0)],
    'Gross margin': ['Your cost of cups sold was ' + m(pl.cogs) + ' on sales of ' + m(pl.rev) + '. What was your gross profit?', m(pl.rev - pl.cogs)],
    'Contribution margin per cup': ['Your average price was ' + m(k.avgPrice) + ' and ingredients cost ' + m(k.costPerCup) + ' a cup. If the cost per cup rose by ' + m(10) + ', what would contribution become?', m(k.avgPrice - k.costPerCup - 10)],
    'Break-even analysis': ['Your daily fixed costs averaged ' + m(F.fixed) + ' and contribution was ' + m(k.contribution) + ' a cup. If the stand fee rose by ' + m(400) + ', how many cups would you need to break even?', (k.contribution > 0 ? Math.ceil((F.fixed + 400) / k.contribution) : '—') + ' cups'],
    'Wealth creation (net worth and retained earnings)': ['Your net worth went from ' + m(s.days[0].nw - s.days[0].np) + ' to ' + m(s.days[n - 1].nw) + ' over ' + n + ' days. What was the average increase a day?', m((s.days[n - 1].nw - s.days[0].nw + s.days[0].np) / n)],
    'Taxation and tax planning': ['You paid ' + m(pl.tax) + ' in tax over ' + n + ' days. How much should you set aside each day on average?', m(pl.tax / n)],
    'Inventory control and waste': ['Your waste and spoilage cost ' + m(pl.wst + pl.sp) + '. If you had halved it, how much more net profit would you have made?', m((pl.wst + pl.sp) / 2)],
    'Conversion rate': ['What share of passers-by bought from you, to the nearest per cent?', pc(k.conversion)],
    'Price experimentation and elasticity': k.elasticity != null ? ['Your measured elasticity was ' + k.elasticity.toFixed(2) + '. If you raised price by 10%, roughly how would the number of buyers change?', 'About ' + Math.round(Math.abs(k.elasticity) * 10) + '% fewer buyers'] : null,
    'Learning curve': k.first7 != null ? ['Your first 7 days averaged ' + m(k.first7) + ' a day and your last 7 days ' + m(k.last7) + '. What was the change?', Math.round(100 * (k.last7 - k.first7) / Math.max(Math.abs(k.first7), 100)) + '%'] : null,
    'Cost of compliance vs cost of non-compliance': ['You paid ' + m(pl.bribe + pl.fine) + ' in fines, penalties and bribes against a ' + m(4000) + ' permit. Which was cheaper over your game?', pl.bribe + pl.fine > 4000 ? 'The permit, by ' + m(pl.bribe + pl.fine - 4000) : 'The fines and bribes, but bribery is illegal and the visits keep coming']
  }[name];
  return X || GENERIC_EX[name] || ['Explain this concept in one sentence and give one example from your own game.', 'See the definition and your numbers on this page.'];
}
var GENERIC_EX = {
  'Working capital and liquidity': ['Why can a business that shows a profit still be unable to pay its supplier tomorrow?', 'Profit counts goods used, not cash paid: cash can be tied up in stock or equipment.'],
  'Cost of debt and leverage': ['A loan of ₹10,000 at 2% a day is repaid after 10 days. How much simple interest is paid?', '₹10,000 × 2% × 10 = ₹2,000'],
  'Choice of lender (cost of capital)': ['How much does ₹5,000 from the moneylender at 20% a day grow to after 5 days, compounded?', '₹5,000 × 1.2⁵ ≈ ₹12,442'],
  'Debt servicing and default risk': ['Daily profit is ₹600 on a normal day and ₹100 on a rainy day. Is an EMI of ₹400 a day safe?', 'No: coverage is 1.5× on normal days but 0.25× on rainy days, so bad weather causes missed payments.'],
  'Production planning (matching supply to demand)': ['You made 60 cups and 48 customers wanted one. What was the match ratio?', '48 ÷ 60 = 80%'],
  'Capacity and stock-outs': ['120 customers wanted lemonade and you could serve 100. What share of demand did you lose?', '20 ÷ 120 ≈ 17%'],
  'Perishable goods (spoilage)': ['Why does first in, first out (FIFO) matter for perishable stock?', 'Older stock is used before it spoils, so less is thrown away.'],
  'Product quality': ['Which two ingredients most affect taste in the game?', 'Lemons and sugar (and ice for the weather).'],
  'Process consistency': ['Taste was 100, 100, 80, 100 on four days. Which day hurt consistency, and what would an SOP fix?', 'The 80 day; a fixed recipe for each weather type.'],
  'Purchasing and supplier deals': ['Sugar is 25% cheaper and keeps for weeks; lemons are 25% cheaper but spoil in 3 days. Which do you stock up on?', 'Sugar: extra lemons would spoil.'],
  'Value-based pricing': ['The fair price on a hot day is ₹60. You charge ₹75. What is your price index, and the risk?', '125%: many passers-by decide not to buy.'],
  'Dynamic pricing (responding to demand)': ['Should price go up or down on a festival day with 80% more people?', 'Up, moderately: demand is higher and capacity is limited.'],
  'Marketing return on investment': ['Ads cost ₹200 and brought 10 extra buyers at ₹36 contribution each. Worth it?', '₹360 − ₹200 = ₹160 gain: yes.'],
  'Targeting marketing spend': ['On which forecasts would you stop advertising, and why?', 'Rainy and stormy: few passers-by, so ads cannot pay back.'],
  'Product differentiation': ['Pink lemonade uses one extra lemon per pitcher but sells higher. When is it worth making?', 'When the extra price per cup covers the extra lemon cost per cup.'],
  'Brand equity and reputation': ['Name three decisions that raise reputation.', 'High taste, fair prices, serving every customer.'],
  'Using forecasts (decisions under uncertainty)': ['The forecast says rainy with a 20% chance of sun. How much ice do you use?', 'Little, as for rain: plan for the most likely outcome.'],
  'Risk management': ['On a storm forecast: open with full stock, open small, or close?', 'Close, or open small with no ads: sales will not cover costs.'],
  'Loss aversion (behavioural bias)': ['Yesterday was rainy and you lost ₹100. Today is sunny. Cut your price?', 'No: the loss came from the weather, not the price.'],
  'Responding to market events': ['A festival brings 80% more people. Name two decisions you would change.', 'Make more pitchers and raise the price slightly.'],
  'Competitive response': ['A rival runs a two-day promotion. Two ways to protect sales?', 'Price slightly lower; keep taste high.'],
  'Capital investment decisions': ['An upgrade costs ₹3,000 and adds ₹150 profit a day. Payback period?', '20 days'],
  'Business growth and scale': ['When is the right time to move to a bigger stand?', 'When customers are often turned away and profit covers the higher fee.'],
  'Equity financing and dilution': ['An investor gives ₹1,00,000 for 30% of profits; profit is ₹2,000 a day. Daily cost of the deal?', '₹600 a day'],
  'Timing of expansion': ['Name two signals that it is time to expand.', 'Frequent stock-outs and a steady cash cushion.'],
  'Return on investment (upgrades)': ['An upgrade cost ₹5,000 and raised profit by ₹100 a day. ROI after 100 days?', '(₹10,000 − ₹5,000) ÷ ₹5,000 = 100%'],
  'Sustainable growth': ['Why is steady growth safer than fast growth on loans?', 'It is paid for by profit, so a bad period does not trigger debt problems.'],
  'Scaling through stand tiers': ['Your stand serves up to 100 cups and you sell 45. Capacity use, and what it suggests?', '45%: demand, not capacity, limits sales; work on price and marketing.'],
  'Workforce planning (staffing to demand)': ['On which days should you hire the helper?', 'Hot and sunny days, festivals and holidays.'],
  'Employee morale': ['One way to protect staff morale in a bad week?', 'Cut losses by closing on storm days; pay wages on time.'],
  'Labour productivity': ['The helper adds 40 cups of capacity at ₹300 a day, but only 5 extra cups sell at ₹36 contribution. Worth it?', '5 × ₹36 = ₹180 < ₹300: no.'],
  'Fixed costs and operating leverage (hiring)': ['Why does a helper make bad-weather days more costly?', 'The wage is paid even when few customers come.'],
  'Regulatory compliance': ['Why is buying the permit early a financial decision as well as an ethical one?', 'It costs once and stops fines and bribes that recur.'],
  'Corruption as a recurring cost': ['An officer asks for ₹1,000; the permit costs ₹2,000. Over 6 visits, which is cheaper?', 'The permit: ₹2,000 once against ₹6,000 (and bribery is illegal).']
};

/* ---------- the student workbook ---------- */
function studentHtml(s, g, all) {
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Kolkata', 'd MMM yyyy'), cur = s.cur, m = function (x) { return money(x, cur); }, mk = s.mk, k = s.k, H = [];
  var RN = new Runner(s.pid, 'Student Workbook', s.name + ' · ' + g.name + ' (' + g.code + ')', today);
  var page = function (part, title, body, flow) { body = kpRows(body);
    if (PDF_MODE) H.push(RN.pdfStart(part, title, flow) + '<div class="part">' + e(part) + '</div><h2>' + e(title) + '</h2>' + body + (flow ? '<div style="height:6mm"></div>' : ''));
    else H.push('<div class="pg' + (flow ? ' flow' : '') + '" style="page:' + RN.page(part, title) + '"><div class="part">' + e(part) + '</div><h2>' + e(title) + '</h2>' + body + '</div>'); };
  var cover = function () { var gr0 = mk ? (mk.inc ? 'I' : mk.gr.g) : '—', gd = all.filter(function (x) { return x.mk && !x.mk.inc; }).length;
    return brandCover('Student Workbook', 'Your graded game, your results and ' + (s.cs ? s.cs.list.length : 45) + ' management concepts explained with your own numbers.', 'Prepared for', s.name, 'Player ID ' + s.pid,
      '<table class="cvgr"><tr><td><b style="font-size:36pt;color:' + (GCOL[gr0] || '#1e3a8a') + '">' + gr0 + '</b><br>Grade</td></tr></table>',
      mk ? [[mk.marks, 'Marks out of 100'], [s.rank ? s.rank + ' of ' + gd : (mk.inc ? 'Incomplete' : '—'), 'Rank in the class'], [s.days.length + ' days', 'Graded attempt']] : [['—', 'Marks out of 100'], ['—', 'Rank in the class'], ['Not graded', 'Needs a game of ' + MIN_DAYS + '+ days']],
      [['Group', g.name + ' (' + g.code + ')'], ['College', s.college || g.college || ''], ['Faculty', g.ownerName || BRAND.by + ', Department of MBA'], ['Report date', today + ' · Grading rules version 2']]); };
  var who = 'The student named on the cover and their institution';
  var head0 = '<h1>' + e(s.name) + '</h1><div class="muted">Player ' + e(s.pid) + ' · ' + e(s.college || g.college) + ' · ' + e(g.name) + ' (' + e(g.code) + ')' + (g.batch ? ' · ' + e(g.batch) : '') +
    '<br>Lemonade Stand: Business Sim · Student workbook · ' + today + '</div>';
  var attRows = s.att.map(function (a) {
    var st = a.n === s.detailN && mk ? 'Graded (best attempt)' : { abandoned: 'Ignored: restarted while the stand still had money', failed: 'Failed: ran out of money (−5)', progress: 'In progress (not counted under 5 days)', current: 'Counted, but another attempt scored higher' }[a.status];
    return '<tr><td>' + a.n + '</td><td>' + e(a.days[0] ? a.days[0].at : '') + ' → ' + e(a.days.length ? a.days[a.days.length - 1].at : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + a.days.length + '</td><td class="n">' + (a.mk ? a.mk.marks : '—') + '</td><td class="n">' + (a.days.length ? m(a.days[a.days.length - 1].nw) : '—') + '</td><td>' + st + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
  var attTable = '<table><tr><th>#</th><th>Played</th><th class="n">Days</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th class="n">Final net worth</th><th>Counted as</th></tr>' + attRows + '</table>';
  if (!mk) { page('Result', 'Not graded yet', head0 + '<h3>Attempts in this group</h3>' + attTable + '<p>Grading needs one game of at least ' + MIN_DAYS + ' days played after joining the group.</p>'); return brandDoc(s.name + ' · Lemonade Stand workbook', RN, cover(), H.join(''), who); }
  var F = attemptFacts(s.days), n = F.n, graded = all.filter(function (x) { return x.mk && !x.mk.inc; });
  var areaB = {}; AREA_ORDER.forEach(function (a) { var v = graded.map(function (x) { var r = areaOf(x, a); return r ? r.m : null; }); areaB[a] = { med: pctile(v, 0.5), top: pctile(v, 0.9), avg: r1(avgOf(v.filter(function (x) { return x != null; }), function (x) { return x; })) }; });
  var cAvg = {}; graded.forEach(function (x) { (x.cs ? x.cs.list : []).forEach(function (c) { (cAvg[c.name] = cAvg[c.name] || []).push(c.s); }); });
  var cav = function (nm) { return cAvg[nm] ? Math.round(avgOf(cAvg[nm], function (x) { return x; })) : '—'; };
  var list = s.cs.list, strong = list.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return b.s - a.s; }).slice(0, 3), weak = list.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.s - b.s; }).slice(0, 3);
  var grd = mk.inc ? 'I' : mk.gr.g, dfB = { easy: 'Easy', normal: 'Normal', hard: 'Hard' }[mk.dif] || 'Normal';
  var mod = s.mod && s.mod.m !== '' ? s.mod : null;
  // Part 1
  var verdict = e(s.name.split(' ')[0]) + ' played ' + n + ' days in the graded attempt, ending with net worth ' + m(s.days[n - 1].nw) + '. Strongest in ' + e(strong[0].name.split(' (')[0]) + ' and ' + e(strong[1].name.split(' (')[0]) +
    '; weakest in ' + e(weak[0].name.split(' (')[0]) + ' and ' + e(weak[1].name.split(' (')[0]) + '.' + (mk.caps.length ? ' Adjustments: ' + e(mk.caps.join('; ')) + '.' : '') +
    (mk.inc ? ' <b>Incomplete:</b> play at least ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days in one game (' + (EVIDENCE_DAYS - n) + ' more) before the reports are made Final.' : '');
  var steps = [['Concepts (A)', r1(mk.A * mk.ev), 'add'], ['Results (B)', r1(mk.B * mk.ev), 'add'], ['Persistence (C)', mk.C, 'add'], ['Before penalties', mk.base, 'total']];
  if (mk.penB) steps.push(['Bribes', mk.penB, 'sub']);
  var capD = r1(mk.base - mk.penB - mk.attempt); if (capD > 0.05) steps.push(['Cap', capD, 'sub']);
  if (mk.failPen) steps.push(['Failed games', mk.failPen, 'sub']);
  steps.push([s.quizPct != null && s.game != null && s.game !== mk.marks ? 'Game marks' : 'Final', r1(Math.max(0, mk.attempt - mk.failPen)), 'total']);
  var p1 = '<table style="border:0"><tr><td style="border:0">' + head0 + '</td><td style="border:0;text-align:right;width:160px"><div class="gr" style="color:' + GCOL[grd] + '">' + grd + '</div><div class="muted">' + mk.marks + ' marks · ' + mk.gr.gp + ' points</div></td></tr></table>' +
    '<div><div class="kp"><b>' + mk.marks + '</b><span>Final marks out of 100</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + (s.rank ? s.rank + ' of ' + graded.length : (mk.inc ? 'Incomplete' : '—')) + '</b><span>Rank in the class</span></div>' +
    '<div class="kp"><b>' + n + ' days</b><span>Graded attempt (' + s.detailN + ' of ' + s.att.length + ')</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + m(mk.avgP) + '</b><span>Profit a day (benchmark ' + m(mk.ben) + ')</span></div></div>' +
    '<div class="bx nt"><b>Summary.</b> ' + verdict + '</div>' + (mod ? '<div class="bx wn"><b>Moderated by faculty: ' + mod.m + ' marks, grade ' + gradeOf(mod.m).g + '.</b> This replaces the calculated marks.' + (mod.c ? ' ' + e(mod.c) : '') + '</div>' : '') +
    '<h3>How the marks were built</h3>' + vis(svBridge(steps), '') +
    '<table><tr><th>Part</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th class="n">Out of</th><th>How</th></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>A. Management concepts</td><td class="n">' + mk.A + '</td><td class="n">65</td><td>' + list.length + ' concepts in ' + mk.areas.length + ' areas, weighted by area</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>B1. Average daily profit</td><td class="n">' + mk.B1 + '</td><td class="n">15</td><td>' + m(mk.avgP) + ' a day against the ' + dfB + ' benchmark ' + m(mk.ben) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>B2. Net worth growth</td><td class="n">' + mk.B2 + '</td><td class="n">10</td><td>' + m(mk.gpd) + ' a day against ' + m(mk.ben) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>C. Persistence</td><td class="n">' + mk.C + '</td><td class="n">10</td><td>' + n + ' days (full marks from ' + FULL_DAYS + ')</td></tr>' +
    (mk.ev < 1 ? '<tr><td>Evidence factor</td><td class="n">× ' + mk.ev.toFixed(2) + '</td><td></td><td>Parts A and B scaled: ' + n + ' of ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days</td></tr>' : '') +
    mk.caps.filter(function (c) { return !/^Evidence/.test(c); }).map(function (c) { return '<tr><td colspan="4">' + e(c) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') +

  page('Part 1 · Result summary', 'Your result', p1);

      ['Interest', pl.int, '#7c3aed'], ['Tax', pl.tax, '#0ea5e9'], ['Investor', pl.inv, '#ec4899'], ['Fines, bribes', pl.fine + pl.bribe, '#7f1d1d'], ['Wear', -pl.other, '#475569'], ['Net profit', pl.np, '#16a34a']].filter(function (x) { return x[1] > 0; }), cur), '') +

  var d0 = s.days[0], dl = s.days[n - 1], low = s.days.filter(function (d) { return d.cash < 1000; }).length;

  // Part 5
  var FAIR = function (w) { return 100 * (WX[w] || [1])[0]; };
  var wx = WXO.filter(function (w) { return F.W[w]; }), Wr = function (w) { var o = F.W[w]; return { pr: o.pr / o.days, sv: o.sv / o.days, conv: o.pb ? o.by / o.pb : 0, np: o.np / o.days, ad: o.ad / o.days }; };
  var badW = ['rainy', 'stormy'].filter(function (w) { return F.W[w]; }), goodW = ['hot', 'sunny', 'cloudy'].filter(function (w) { return F.W[w]; });
  var over = goodW.length ? avgOf(goodW, function (w) { return Wr(w).pr / FAIR(w) - 1; }) : 0;
  page('Part 5 · Decision analytics', 'Unit economics and pricing', '<h3>Unit economics</h3><div><div class="kp"><b>' + m(k.avgPrice) + '</b><span>Average price a cup</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + m(k.costPerCup) + '</b><span>Ingredient cost a cup</span></div>' +

Math.ceil(F.fixed / k.contribution) : '—') + ' cups</b><span>Break-even a day (you sold ' + Math.round(k.avgCups) + ')</span></div></div>' +

' On ' + badW.map(function (w) { return WXN[w].toLowerCase(); }).join(' and ') + ' days profit averaged ' + badW.map(function (w) { return m(Wr(w).np); }).join(' and ') + ' a day' + (badW.some(function (w) { return Wr(w).ad > 0; }) ? ', with advertising still running' : '') + '.' : '') + '</div>');

  // Part 7
  var kp = function (f) { return graded.map(function (x) { return x.k && x.days.length ? f(x) : null; }); };
  var bm = [['Profit a day', function (x) { return x.mk.avgP; }, m, 1], ['Net profit margin', function (x) { var r = x.days.reduce(function (t, d) { return t + d.rev; }, 0); return r ? x.k.profit / r : null; }, pc, 1],

  // Part 8
  var pri = [];
  if (k.bribes) pri.push(['Buy the Street Vending Permit at the first officer visit', '0 bribes', k.bribes + ' bribes cost ' + mk.penB + ' marks and ' + m(pl.bribe)]);
  if (s.days.some(function (d) { return d.rj > 0; })) pri.push(['Never borrow from the moneylender', 'No moneylender days', 'At 20% a day the debt more than doubles in 4 days']);
  if (mk.ev < 1) pri.push(['Play at least ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days in one game', EVIDENCE_DAYS + '+ days', 'Short games scale Parts A and B down']);
  weak.forEach(function (c) { if (pri.length < 3) pri.push([c.tip, c.name.split(' (')[0] + ' above 60', c.bad || 'Your weakest concepts']); });
  page('Part 8 · Improvement plan', 'Your improvement plan', '<h3>Three priorities for your next game</h3><table><tr><th>#</th><th>Priority</th><th>Target</th><th>Why</th></tr>' + pri.slice(0, 3).map(function (p, i) { return '<tr><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td>' + e(p[0]) + '</td><td>' + e(p[1]) + '</td><td>' + e(p[2]) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' +
    '<h3>Daily decision checklist</h3><ol><li>Read the forecast and any event: is today busy, normal or slow?</li><li>Busy: more pitchers, price about 5–10% above fair, helper on. Slow: fewer pitchers, no ads, no helper. Storm: consider closing.</li>' +
    '<li>Recipe: keep taste above 90; set ice by the weather.</li><li>Check break-even cups against the expected crowd before spending on ads.</li><li>Keep one day\'s stock money in cash; avoid the moneylender; buy the permit early.</li></ol>' +
    '<h3>Reflection questions</h3><ol><li>Which single decision would have raised your marks the most, and by how much?</li><li>Compare your best and worst day: what did you do differently?</li><li>Was every upgrade and hire worth its cost? Use your own numbers.</li><li>Where did your price differ most from the fair price, and what happened to sales?</li><li>What will you do differently in your next game?</li></ol>');
  // Part 9
  page('Part 9 · Formula sheet', 'Formula sheet', '<p class="sm muted">Definitions are on each concept\'s page in Part 6.</p><table style="font-size:8.5pt"><tr><th style="width:34%">Term</th><th>Formula</th></tr>' +
    Object.keys(NOTES).sort().map(function (nm) { return '<tr><td><b>' + e(nm.split(' (')[0]) + '</b></td><td>' + e(NOTES[nm].f) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>', true);
  page('Part 9 · How marks were worked out', 'Marks calculation and moderation', '<ol><li><b>Graded attempt:</b> attempt ' + s.detailN + ' (' + n + ' days), your best attempt of ' + MIN_DAYS + ' or more days. ' +
    s.att.filter(function (a) { return a.n !== s.detailN; }).map(function (a) { return 'Attempt ' + a.n + ': ' + { abandoned: 'restarted while solvent, ignored', failed: 'ran out of money, −5', progress: 'in progress', current: 'counted, lower marks' }[a.status]; }).join('; ') + '.</li>' +
    '<li><b>Part A, concepts:</b> area = average of its concept scores; areas weighted ' + AREA_ORDER.map(function (a) { return a.split(' ')[0] + ' ' + AREA_W[a]; }).join(', ') + ' (of 65). Yours: ' + mk.A + '.</li>' +
    '<li><b>Part B, results:</b> profit ' + m(mk.avgP) + ' a day → ' + mk.B1 + ' of 15; net worth growth ' + m(mk.gpd) + ' a day → ' + mk.B2 + ' of 10 (' + dfB + ' benchmark ' + m(mk.ben) + ').</li>' +
    '<li><b>Part C, persistence:</b> ' + n + ' days → ' + mk.C + ' of 10.</li><li><b>Evidence factor:</b> × ' + mk.ev.toFixed(2) + (mk.ev < 1 ? ' (fewer than ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days)' : '') + '.</li>' +
    '<li><b>Penalties and caps:</b> ' + (mk.caps.filter(function (c) { return !/^Evidence|^Quiz/.test(c); }).map(e).join('; ') || 'none') + '.</li>' +
    (mk.caps.some(function (c) { return /^Quiz/.test(c); }) ? '<li><b>Quiz:</b> ' + e(mk.caps.filter(function (c) { return /^Quiz/.test(c); })[0]) + '.</li>' : '') +
    '<li><b>Final:</b> ' + mk.marks + ', grade ' + grd + (mk.inc ? ' (Incomplete until ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days or until reports are Final)' : ' (' + mk.gr.gp + ' points)') + '.</li></ol>' +
    '<div class="bx"><b>Faculty moderation:</b> ' + (mod ? mod.m + ' marks, grade ' + gradeOf(mod.m).g + (mod.c ? '. ' + e(mod.c).replace(/\n/g, '<br>') : '') : '<span class="muted">none.</span>') + '</div>');
  // Appendix
  var dr = function (d) { return '<tr><td class="n">' + d.d + '</td><td>' + (d.cl ? 'closed' : (WXN[d.w] || d.w)) + '</td><td>' + (d.pd || '') + ' ' + d.le + '/' + d.su + '/' + d.ic + '</td><td class="n">' + m(d.pr) + '</td><td class="n">' + d.pb + '</td><td class="n">' + d.by + '</td><td class="n">' + d.sv + '</td><td class="n">' + d.ta + '</td><td class="n">' + m(d.ad || 0) + '</td><td class="n">' + m(d.np) + '</td><td>' + e(EVN[d.ev] || d.ev || '') + '</td></tr>'; };
  var hdr = '<tr><th class="n">Day</th><th>Weather</th><th>Recipe</th><th class="n">Price</th><th class="n">Passers</th><th class="n">Buyers</th><th class="n">Served</th><th class="n">Turned away</th><th class="n">Ads</th><th class="n">Profit</th><th>Event</th></tr>';
  var showAll = n <= 60;
  page('Appendix A', 'Day-by-day decisions', showAll ? '<table class="sm">' + hdr + s.days.map(dr).join('') + '</table>' :
    '<h3>First 10 days</h3><table class="sm">' + hdr + s.days.slice(0, 10).map(dr).join('') + '</table><h3>Latest 15 days</h3><table class="sm">' + hdr + s.days.slice(-15).map(dr).join('') + '</table><p class="sm muted">The other days are summarised in blocks in Part 3.</p>');
  page('Appendix B', 'Answers to the self-check exercises', '<table class="sm"><tr><th>#</th><th>Concept</th><th>Question</th><th>Answer</th></tr>' + exk.map(function (x, i) { return '<tr><td>' + (i + 1) + '</td><td>' + e(x[0].split(' (')[0]) + '</td><td>' + e(x[1]) + '</td><td>' + e(x[2]) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>');
  return brandDoc(s.name + ' · Lemonade Stand workbook', RN, cover(), H.join(''), who);
}
/* ---------- Branding (22 Nov 2026): cover, contents, running header and footer, training page, copyright page ----------
 * Two outputs from the same HTML pieces (tables only, so both lay them out the same):
 *  - browser print (Apps Script Drive PDF, and the panel's preview): header/footer as @page margin boxes on named pages;
 *  - PDF_MODE (the panel's "Report PDF", made on the server by mPDF, admin/makepdf.php): <pagebreak page-selector>, <htmlpageheader>,
 *    <htmlpagefooter> with "Page {PAGENO} of {nbpg}", and a contents page with real page numbers (<tocpagebreak>, <tocentry>). */

var PDF_CSS = '<style>@page{margin:0}@page brandcv{margin:0;header:_blank;footer:_blank}body{font-family:dejavusanscondensed;font-size:9.6pt}' +
  '.fm{font-family:dejavusansmono}table.rh{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:0;border-bottom:1.5pt solid #1e3a8a}table.rh td{border:0;padding:0 0 2mm;font-size:7.5pt;color:#111827;vertical-align:bottom}table.rh td.l{color:#1e3a8a;font-weight:700}' +

  'div.mpdf_toc{font-family:dejavusanscondensed}a.mpdf_toc_a{color:#111827;text-decoration:none}div.mpdf_toc_level_0{padding:2.4mm 0 0;font-weight:700;font-size:11pt;border-bottom:0}div.mpdf_toc_level_1{padding:.4mm 0 0 8mm;font-size:9.5pt;color:#374151}' +
  'span.mpdf_toc_t_level_0{font-weight:700}span.mpdf_toc_p_level_0{font-weight:700;color:#1e3a8a}span.mpdf_toc_p_level_1{color:#374151}' +
  '.cvfoot{position:absolute;bottom:11mm;left:17mm;width:176mm}.bkp{position:absolute;bottom:2.6mm;left:0;width:176mm;padding:11mm 17mm 12mm}.ambb{position:absolute;bottom:0;left:0;width:210mm}' +
  'span.mpdf_toc_t_level_1{font-style:normal;font-weight:normal}span.mpdf_toc_t_level_0{font-style:normal}</style>';
function cssStr(x) { return '"' + String(x).replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/"/g, '\\"').replace(/[\r\n]+/g, ' ') + '"'; }
function kpRows(h) {   // KPI boxes as a table, rows of 4 (tables lay out the same in the browser and in mPDF)
  return h.replace(/<div[^>]*>((?:<div class="kp">[\s\S]*?<\/div>)+)<\/div>/g, function (all, inner) {
    var cells = inner.match(/<div class="kp">[\s\S]*?<\/div>/g).map(function (c) { return '<td class="kp">' + c.slice(16, -6).replace('</b><span>', '</b><br><span>') + '</td>'; }), rows = '';
    for (var i = 0; i < cells.length; i += 4) rows += '<tr>' + cells.slice(i, i + 4).join('') + (i + 4 > cells.length ? new Array(i + 5 - cells.length).join('<td class="kp0"></td>') : '') + '</tr>';
    return '<table class="kpt">' + rows + '</table>'; });
}
function Runner(id, title, who, date) { this.id = String(id).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g, '') || 'x'; this.title = title; this.who = who; this.date = date; this.rules = []; this.heads = []; this.names = {}; this.rec = []; this.lastKey = null; this.lastFlow = false; }
Runner.prototype.page = function (part, title) {
  this.rec.push([part, title]); var n = this.names[part];
  if (!n) {
    n = this.names[part] = 'r' + this.id + 'p' + this.rules.length;
    var head = this.title + (part ? ' · ' + part.replace(/ \(continued\)$/, '') : '');
    if (PDF_MODE) {
      this.rules.push('@page ' + n + '{margin:21mm 14mm 20mm;margin-header:8mm;margin-footer:6mm;header:html_' + n + ';footer:html_ft}');
      this.heads.push('<htmlpageheader name="' + n + '"><table class="rh"><tr><td class="l">' + e(head) + '</td><td style="text-align:right">' + e(this.who) + '</td></tr></table></htmlpageheader>');
    } else {
      var f = 'font-family:"Segoe UI",Arial,sans-serif;font-size:7.5pt;', top = f + 'vertical-align:bottom;padding-bottom:2.5mm;border-bottom:1.5pt solid #1e3a8a;',
        bot = 'font-family:"Segoe UI",Arial,sans-serif;font-size:6.8pt;line-height:1.45;vertical-align:top;padding-top:2.2mm;border-top:1pt solid #1e3a8a;color:#4b5563;';
      this.rules.push('@page ' + n + '{size:A4;margin:21mm 14mm 17mm;@top-left{content:' + cssStr(head) + ';' + top + 'color:#1e3a8a;font-weight:700}' +
        '@top-center{content:"";' + top + '}@top-right{content:' + cssStr(this.who) + ';' + top + 'color:#111827}' +
        '@bottom-left{content:url("' + FOOT_IMG + '");' + bot + '}' +
        '@bottom-center{content:' + cssStr('Grading rules v2 · ' + this.date) + ' "\\A Page " counter(page);white-space:pre;text-align:center;' + bot + '}' +
        '@bottom-right{content:' + cssStr(BRAND.site + ' · Training: ' + BRAND.phone) + ' "\\A " ' + cssStr('© ' + new Date().getFullYear() + ' IndianResearchers.com. All rights reserved.') + ';white-space:pre;text-align:right;' + bot + '}}');
    }
  }
  return n;
};
// PDF_MODE: the tags that start one report page: a page break (unless the previous page flows on) and its contents-page entries
Runner.prototype.pdfStart = function (part, title, flow) {
  var n = this.page(part, title), p = part.replace(/ \(continued\)$/, ''), i = p.indexOf(' · '), key = i > 0 ? p.slice(0, i) : p, name = i > 0 ? p.slice(i + 3) : title, out = '';
  if (!this.lastFlow) out += '<pagebreak page-selector="' + n + '" />';
  if (key !== this.lastKey) out += '<tocentry content="' + e(key && key !== name ? key + ' · ' + name : name) + '" level="0" />';
  out += '@@TOC' + (this.rec.length - 1) + '@@';
  this.lastKey = key; this.lastFlow = !!flow; return out;
};
Runner.prototype.css = function () { return '<style>' + this.rules.join('') + '</style>'; };
Runner.prototype.footer = function () {
  var y = new Date().getFullYear();
  return '<htmlpagefooter name="ft"><table class="rf"><tr><td style="width:36%"><span style="font-size:5.6pt;letter-spacing:1pt;color:#6b7280">A PRODUCT OF</span><br>' +
    '<b style="font-size:10pt;color:#1e3a8a">IndianResearchers</b><b style="font-size:10pt;color:#b45309">.com</b></td>' +
    '<td style="width:22%;text-align:center">Grading rules v2 · ' + e(this.date) + '<br><b style="color:#1e3a8a">Page {PAGENO} of {nbpg}</b></td>' +
    '<td style="text-align:right">' + BRAND.site + ' · Training: ' + BRAND.phone + '<br>© ' + y + ' IndianResearchers.com. All rights reserved.</td></tr></table></htmlpagefooter>';
};
function brandMark(px) { return vis('<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="' + px + '" height="' + px + '" viewBox="0 0 40 40"><circle cx="20" cy="20" r="18" fill="#fcd34d"/><circle cx="20" cy="20" r="12" fill="none" stroke="#1e3a8a" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M20 8V32M8 20H32M11.5 11.5L28.5 28.5M28.5 11.5L11.5 28.5" stroke="#1e3a8a" stroke-width="1.6"/></svg>', ''); }
function brandLine(px) { return '<table class="bl" style="width:120mm"><tr>' + (SVG_OK ? '<td style="width:' + (px + 12) + 'px">' + brandMark(px) + '</td>' : '') + '<td style="color:#fff;font-weight:700;font-size:12pt">Lemonade Stand: Business Sim</td></tr></table>'; }
function brandProduct() { return '<span class="prodl">A product of</span><br><span class="prodb">IndianResearchers</span><span class="prodc">.com</span>'; }
function brandPage(inner) { return PDF_MODE ? '<pagebreak page-selector="brandcv" />' + inner : '<div class="pg cv">' + inner + '</div>'; }
function brandCover(kind, tag, whoLab, whoName, whoSub, badge, kpis, details) {
  var w = kind.split(' '), inner = '<div class="cvtop">' + brandLine(40) + '<div class="kick">Management skills report</div><div class="cvh">' + w.slice(0, -1).join(' ') + '<br>' + w[w.length - 1] + '</div><div class="cvtag">' + e(tag) + '</div></div><div class="amb">&nbsp;</div>' +
    '<div class="cvbody"><table class="cvwho"><tr><td><div class="lab">' + whoLab + '</div><div class="cvname">' + e(whoName) + '</div><div class="muted">' + e(whoSub) + '</div></td><td style="text-align:right;width:40mm">' + badge + '</td></tr></table>' +
    '<table class="cvk"><tr>' + kpis.map(function (k) { return '<td style="width:' + Math.floor(100 / kpis.length) + '%"><b style="font-size:17pt;color:#111827">' + k[0] + '</b><br><span style="font-size:9pt;color:#4b5563">' + e(k[1]) + '</span></td>'; }).join('') + '</tr></table>' +
    '<table class="cvd">' + details.map(function (d) { return '<tr><td class="k">' + e(d[0]) + '</td><td>' + e(d[1]) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div>' +
    '<div class="cvfoot"><table class="ft2"><tr><td>' + brandProduct() + '</td><td class="rt">' + BRAND.site + '<br>Training: ' + BRAND.phone + '<br>© ' + new Date().getFullYear() + ' IndianResearchers.com. All rights reserved.</td></tr></table></div>';
  return PDF_MODE ? inner : '<div class="pg cv">' + inner + '</div>';   // the PDF starts on the cover page (default @page, no margins)
}
function brandToc(RN) {   // browser version (the PDF builds its own with page numbers)
  var groups = [], by = {};
  RN.rec.forEach(function (r) {
    var part = r[0].replace(/ \(continued\)$/, ''), i = part.indexOf(' · '), key = i > 0 ? part.slice(0, i) : part, name = i > 0 ? part.slice(i + 3) : r[1], gk = by[key];
    if (!gk) { gk = by[key] = { key: key, name: name, subs: [], n: 0 }; groups.push(gk); }
    gk.n++; if (r[1] && gk.subs.indexOf(r[1]) < 0) gk.subs.push(r[1]);
  });
  groups.push({ key: '', name: 'About the designer and training programmes', subs: [], n: 1 }, { key: '', name: 'Copyright and disclaimers', subs: [], n: 1 });
  return '<table class="toc">' + groups.map(function (gk) {
    return '<tr><td class="k">' + e(gk.key) + '</td><td class="t">' + e(gk.name) + (gk.n > 1 ? '<div class="sub">' + gk.subs.map(e).join(' · ') + '</div>' : '') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' +
    '<p class="sm muted" style="margin-top:5mm">Each part starts on a new page.</p>';
}
function brandTraining(RN) {
  var part = 'About the designer and training', start = PDF_MODE ? '<pagebreak page-selector="' + RN.page(part, '') + '" /><tocentry content="About the designer and training programmes" level="0" />' : '<div class="pg" style="page:' + RN.page(part, '') + '">';
  RN.rec.pop();
  return start + '<div class="trtop"><table class="trt"><tr><td style="width:42mm"><img src="' + AUTHOR_IMG + '" style="width:36mm;height:36mm" alt="' + BRAND.by + '"></td><td><div class="kick">Simulation designed and conceptualised by</div>' +
    '<div class="trname">' + BRAND.by + '</div><div class="trpost">' + BRAND.post + '<br>Founder, IndianResearchers.com</div></td></tr></table></div>' +
    '<div class="kick" style="margin-top:7mm">Training programmes</div><h2 style="border:0;margin:1mm 0 2mm">Bring Lemonade Stand to your classroom</h2>' +
    '<p>Hands-on sessions where students run a business, make decisions every day and learn management by doing. Available for colleges, universities and corporate teams, on campus or online.</p>' +
    '<table class="trg"><tr><td><b>Student workshops</b><br>Live game sessions for MBA, BBA and commerce classes, with a projector leaderboard, market events and individual graded reports.</td>' +
    '<td><b>Faculty development programmes</b><br>How to teach with business simulations: running a class group, class controls, quizzes and reading the analytics.</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td><b>Competitions and events</b><br>Inter-class and inter-college business games for management fests, with live rankings and certificates.</td>' +
    '<td><b>Corporate and entrepreneurship training</b><br>Decision-making, pricing, cash management and risk, for new managers and first-time founders.</td></tr></table>' +
    '<h3>Every programme includes</h3><table class="inc"><tr><td><span class="tick">✓</span> A private class group on the app</td><td><span class="tick">✓</span> Graded workbook for every student</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td><span class="tick">✓</span> Faculty group report with class analytics</td><td><span class="tick">✓</span> Certificates of participation and merit</td></tr></table>' +
    '<table class="book"><tr><td><b style="font-size:13pt">Book a session or ask a question</b><br><span class="muted">Students, faculty and institutions are welcome to get in touch.</span></td>' +
    '<td class="r"><b>Phone: ' + BRAND.phone + '</b><br><b>' + BRAND.email + '</b><br>' + BRAND.site + '</td></tr></table>' + (PDF_MODE ? '' : '</div>');
}
function brandBack(who) {
  var y = new Date().getFullYear();
  return brandPage((PDF_MODE ? '<tocentry content="Copyright and disclaimers" level="0" />' : '') + '<div class="bkin"><div class="kick">Copyright notice</div><h2>© ' + y + ' IndianResearchers.com. All rights reserved.</h2>' +
    '<p>Lemonade Stand: Business Sim, and this report\'s design, text, concept notes, exercises, charts and grading method, are the intellectual property of ' + BRAND.by + ' and IndianResearchers.com. ' +
    'No part of this report may be copied, reproduced, published, sold or shared in any form without written permission. ' + who + ' may keep, print and use it for academic purposes.</p>' +
    '<div class="kick" style="margin-top:5mm">Disclaimers</div><table class="dis"><tr>' +
    '<td><b>For education only</b><br>This is a learning simulation. Results in the game do not predict real business results, and nothing here is financial, investment, tax or legal advice.</td>' +
    '<td><b>Fictional scenarios</b><br>People, lenders, officers and events in the game are fictional. Unethical choices such as bribery are shown only to teach their cost, and are never endorsed.</td></tr><tr>' +
    '<td><b>Marks and grades</b><br>Marks are worked out automatically from the student\'s own game data. The faculty member may moderate them, and the institution\'s own evaluation rules prevail.</td>' +
    '<td><b>Accuracy</b><br>The report is provided as is. Care is taken over every figure; if you find an error, please report it to ' + BRAND.email + '.</td></tr></table>' +
    '<p style="font-size:9pt"><b>Your data.</b> Game data is used for this report as described in the privacy policy at ' + BRAND.privacy + '. To see or delete your data, email ' + BRAND.email + '.</p></div>' +
    '<div class="bkp">' + brandLine(36) + '<div class="tx">Training programmes for colleges, faculty and companies. Call <b style="color:#fff">' + BRAND.phone + '</b> or email <b style="color:#fff">' + BRAND.email + '</b>.</div>' +
    '<table class="bkrow"><tr><td><div class="kick" style="color:#fcd34d">A product of</div><div class="big" style="font-weight:bold">IndianResearchers.com</div><div style="font-size:9.5pt;color:#dbe4ff">' + BRAND.site + '</div></td>' +
    '<td class="rb">Designed by ' + BRAND.by + '<br>© ' + y + ' IndianResearchers.com</td></tr></table></div><div class="ambb">&nbsp;</div>');
}
function brandDoc(title, RN, cover, body, who) {
  if (PDF_MODE) {
    var cnt = {}, keyOf = RN.rec.map(function (r) { var p = r[0].replace(/ \(continued\)$/, ''), i = p.indexOf(' · '); return i > 0 ? p.slice(0, i) : p; });
    keyOf.forEach(function (k) { cnt[k] = (cnt[k] || 0) + 1; });
    body = body.replace(/@@TOC(\d+)@@/g, function (all, i) { var r = RN.rec[+i]; return cnt[keyOf[+i]] > 1 && r[1] ? '<tocentry content="' + e(r[1]) + '" level="1" />' : ''; });
    var tn = RN.page('Contents', ''); RN.rec.pop();
    var first = /<pagebreak page-selector="([^"]+)" \/>/.exec(body), sel1 = first ? first[1] : tn; if (first) body = body.replace(first[0], '');
    var train = brandTraining(RN);
    return '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>' + e(title) + '</title>' + WB_CSS.replace(/@page\{[^}]*\}/, '') + BR_CSS.replace(/@page brandcv\{[^}]*\}/, '') + PDF_CSS + RN.css() + '</head><body>' + RN.heads.join('') + RN.footer() + cover +
      '<tocpagebreak page-selector="' + sel1 + '" toc-page-selector="' + tn + '" links="on" toc-preHTML="' + e('<h2 style="font-size:22pt">Contents</h2>') + '" />' +
      body + train + brandBack(who) + '</body></html>';
  }
  var toc = '<div class="pg" style="page:' + RN.page('Contents', '') + '"><h2 style="font-size:22pt">Contents</h2>' + '@@TOC@@' + '</div>';
  RN.rec.pop();
  var tr = brandTraining(RN);
  return '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>' + e(title) + '</title>' + WB_CSS + BR_CSS + RN.css() + '</head><body>' + cover + toc.replace('@@TOC@@', brandToc(RN)) + body + tr + brandBack(who) + '</body></html>';
}
function conceptArea(nm) { return { 'Taxation and tax planning': 'Finance and accounting', 'Responding to market events': 'Economics and decision-making', 'Scaling through stand tiers': 'Strategy and growth' }[nm] || ''; }
function gradeSpread(students) {
  var dist = {}; GRADES.concat([[0, 'I', 0]]).forEach(function (g) { dist[g[1]] = 0; }); students.forEach(function (s) { if (s.mk) dist[s.mk.inc ? 'I' : s.mk.gr.g]++; });
  var keys = GRADES.map(function (g) { return g[1]; }).concat(dist.I ? ['I'] : []);
  return vis(svCols(keys, [keys.map(function (k) { return dist[k]; })], ['Students'], ['#9ca3af'], function (v) { return v; }), '') +
    '<table style="width:70%"><tr>' + keys.map(function (k) { return '<th style="text-align:center;color:' + GCOL[k] + '">' + k + '</th>'; }).join('') + '</tr><tr>' + keys.map(function (k) { return '<td style="text-align:center">' + dist[k] + '</td>'; }).join('') + '</tr></table>';
}

/* ---------- faculty group report ---------- */
function groupHtml(g, students) {
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Kolkata', 'd MMM yyyy'), H = [], m = function (x) { return money(x, 'INR'); };
  var RN = new Runner('g' + g.code, 'Faculty Group Report', g.name + ' (' + g.code + ')', today);
  var page = function (part, title, body) { body = kpRows(body);
    if (PDF_MODE) H.push(RN.pdfStart(part, title) + '<div class="part">' + e(part) + '</div><h2>' + e(title) + '</h2>' + body);
    else H.push('<div class="pg" style="page:' + RN.page(part, title) + '"><div class="part">' + e(part) + '</div><h2>' + e(title) + '</h2>' + body + '</div>'); };
  var all = students.filter(function (s) { return s.mk; }), gr = all.filter(function (s) { return !s.mk.inc; }), inc = all.filter(function (s) { return s.mk.inc; }), st = classStats(students);
  var ng = students.filter(function (s) { return !s.mk; });
  var head0 = '<h1>' + e(g.name) + '</h1><div class="muted">' + e(g.code) + ' · ' + e(g.college) + (g.batch ? ' · ' + e(g.batch) : '') + (g.ownerName ? ' · ' + e(g.ownerName) : '') + ' · Faculty group report · ' + today + ' · ' + (REP_FINAL ? 'reports Final' : 'draft (Incomplete students not yet graded)') + '</div>';
  var cover = function () { return brandCover('Faculty Group Report', 'Class results, concept analytics, behaviour and risk, and teaching recommendations for the group.', 'Prepared for', g.name, g.code + (g.batch ? ' · ' + g.batch : ''),
      '<table class="cvgr"><tr><td><b style="font-size:36pt;color:#1e3a8a">' + st.n + '</b><br>Graded</td></tr></table>', [[students.length, 'Students in the group'], [st.n ? st.mean : '—', 'Average marks'], [st.n ? st.pass : '—', 'Pass rate (40+)'], [inc.length, 'Incomplete']],
      [['College', g.college || ''], ['Faculty', g.ownerName || BRAND.by + ', Department of MBA'], ['Report status', REP_FINAL ? 'Final' : 'Draft (Incomplete students not yet graded)'], ['Report date', today + ' · Grading rules version 2']]); };
  var who = 'The faculty member and institution named on the cover';
  if (!st.n) { page('Part 1', 'Class dashboard', head0 + '<p>No student is graded yet (each needs a game of ' + MIN_DAYS + ' or more days).</p>'); return brandDoc(g.name + ' · group report', RN, cover(), H.join(''), who); }
  var marks = gr.map(function (s) { return s.mk.marks; }), hist = []; for (var b = 0; b < 100; b += 10) hist.push(marks.filter(function (x) { return x >= b && (x < b + 10 || (b === 90 && x <= 100)); }).length);
  page('Part 1 · Class dashboard', 'Class dashboard', head0 + '<div style="margin-top:8px"><div class="kp"><b>' + students.length + '</b><span>Students in the group</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + st.n + '</b><span>Graded</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + inc.length + '</b><span>Incomplete (under ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days, below 40)</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + ng.length + '</b><span>Not graded (no 5-day game)</span></div>' +
    '<div class="kp"><b>' + st.mean + '</b><span>Average marks</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + st.median + '</b><span>Median marks</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + st.pass + '</b><span>Pass rate (40+)</span></div><div class="kp"><b>' + st.gp + '</b><span>Average grade points</span></div></div>' +
    '<h3>Grade distribution</h3>' + gradeSpread(students) + '<h3>Marks histogram (graded students)</h3>' + vis(svCols(['0–9', '10–19', '20–29', '30–39', '40–49', '50–59', '60–69', '70–79', '80–89', '90–100'], [hist], ['Students'], ['#2563eb'], function (v) { return v; }), ''));
  var avg = function (f) { return r1(avgOf(gr, f)); }, aA = avg(function (s) { return s.mk.A * s.mk.ev; }), aB = avg(function (s) { return s.mk.B * s.mk.ev; }), aC = avg(function (s) { return s.mk.C; }), aP = avg(function (s) { return s.mk.penB; }), aF = avg(function (s) { return s.mk.failPen; });
  page('Part 2 · Marks build-up', 'Where the class\'s marks come from', vis(svBridge([['Concepts (A)', aA, 'add'], ['Results (B)', aB, 'add'], ['Persistence (C)', aC, 'add'], ['Before penalties', r1(aA + aB + aC), 'total'], ['Bribes', aP, 'sub'], ['Failed games', aF, 'sub'], ['Average', st.mean, 'total']]), '') +
    '<table><tr><th>Part</th><th class="n">Class average</th><th class="n">Out of</th></tr><tr><td>A. Concepts (after evidence factor)</td><td class="n">' + aA + '</td><td class="n">65</td></tr><tr><td>B. Results (after evidence factor)</td><td class="n">' + aB + '</td><td class="n">25</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>C. Persistence</td><td class="n">' + aC + '</td><td class="n">10</td></tr><tr><td>Bribe penalties</td><td class="n">−' + aP + '</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>Failed-game penalties</td><td class="n">−' + aF + '</td><td></td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Capped (5+ bribes, seizure, jail)</td><td class="n">' + gr.filter(function (s) { return s.mk.bribes >= 5 || s.mk.seized || s.mk.jail; }).length + ' students</td><td></td></tr></table>' +
    '<div class="bx nt">The class loses most marks in <b>Part B</b> when its average is far below 25: that means many students made little or no profit a day, regardless of decision quality.</div>');
  var byC = {}; gr.forEach(function (s) { (s.cs ? s.cs.list : []).forEach(function (c) { (byC[c.name] = byC[c.name] || { area: c.area, v: [] }).v.push(c.s); }); });
  var cn = Object.keys(byC).map(function (nm) { var v = byC[nm].v; return { nm: nm, area: byC[nm].area, avg: r1(avgOf(v, function (x) { return x; })), n: v.length, low: v.filter(function (x) { return x < 50; }).length }; }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.avg - b.avg; });
  var ar = AREA_ORDER.map(function (a) { var v = gr.map(function (s) { return areaOf(s, a); }).filter(Boolean).map(function (x) { return x.m; }); return v.length ? { a: a, avg: r1(avgOf(v, function (x) { return x; })), med: pctile(v, 0.5), top: pctile(v, 0.9), n: v.length } : null; }).filter(Boolean);
  page('Part 3 · Area and concept analysis', 'What the class understands, and what it does not', vis(svBars(ar.map(function (x) { return [x.a, x.avg, [x.med, x.top]]; })), hbars(ar.map(function (x) { return [x.a, x.avg, x.avg + '', GCOL[gradeOf(x.avg).g]]; }))) +
    '<p class="sm muted">Bars = class average; black tick = median; green tick = top 10%.</p><h3>The 10 weakest concepts</h3><table><tr><th>Concept</th><th>Area</th><th class="n">Class average</th><th class="n">Students below 50</th><th>Teaching note</th></tr>' +
    cn.slice(0, 10).map(function (c) { return '<tr><td>' + e(c.nm) + '</td><td class="sm">' + e(c.area.split(' ')[0]) + '</td><td class="n"><b>' + c.avg + '</b></td><td class="n">' + c.low + ' of ' + c.n + '</td><td class="sm">' + e((NOTES[c.nm] || {}).full ? 'Full marks need: ' + NOTES[c.nm].full.toLowerCase() : '') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' +
    '<h3>All concepts, weakest first</h3><table class="strip"><tr><th>Concept</th><th class="n">Students</th><th class="n">Average</th><th class="n">Below 50</th></tr>' + cn.map(function (c) { return '<tr><td>' + e(c.nm) + '</td><td class="n">' + c.n + '</td><td class="n">' + c.avg + '</td><td class="n">' + c.low + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>');
  var kv = function (f) { return gr.map(f).filter(function (x) { return x != null && !isNaN(x); }); };
  var krow = function (lab, f, fmt) { var v = kv(f); return '<tr><td>' + lab + '</td><td class="n">' + fmt(pctile(v, 0.1)) + '</td><td class="n">' + fmt(pctile(v, 0.5)) + '</td><td class="n">' + fmt(pctile(v, 0.9)) + '</td></tr>'; };
  page('Part 4 · Business results', 'How the businesses performed', '<table><tr><th>Measure (graded attempt)</th><th class="n">Bottom 10%</th><th class="n">Median</th><th class="n">Top 10%</th></tr>' +
    krow('Profit a day', function (s) { return s.mk.avgP; }, m) + krow('Net worth growth a day', function (s) { return s.mk.gpd; }, m) + krow('Net profit margin', function (s) { var r = s.days.reduce(function (t, d) { return t + d.rev; }, 0); return r ? s.k.profit / r : null; }, pc) +
    krow('Waste ratio', function (s) { return s.k.waste; }, pc) + krow('Taste', function (s) { return s.k.taste; }, function (v) { return v == null ? '—' : Math.round(v); }) + krow('Conversion', function (s) { return s.k.conversion; }, pc) +
    krow('Average price', function (s) { return s.k.avgPrice; }, m) + krow('Days played', function (s) { return s.days.length; }, function (v) { return v == null ? '—' : v; }) + '</table>' +
    '<p>' + gr.filter(function (s) { return s.mk.avgP <= 0; }).length + ' of ' + gr.length + ' graded students lost money on an average day; ' + gr.filter(function (s) { return s.mk.avgP >= s.mk.ben; }).length + ' reached the profit benchmark.</p>');
  var who = function (f) { var l = all.filter(f); return l.length + (l.length ? ': ' + l.slice(0, 12).map(function (s) { return e(s.name); }).join(', ') + (l.length > 12 ? ' and ' + (l.length - 12) + ' more' : '') : ''); };
  var used = function (s, f) { return s.days.some(f); };
  page('Part 5 · Behaviour and risk', 'Behaviour and risk', '<table><tr><th>Behaviour (graded attempt)</th><th>Students</th></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Borrowed from the moneylender</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return used(s, function (d) { return d.rj > 0; }); }) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Equipment seized by a lender</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return s.mk.seized; }) + '</td></tr><tr><td>Went to jail</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return s.mk.jail; }) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Failed games (ran out of money)</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return s.failed > 0; }) + '</td></tr><tr><td>Paid 5 or more bribes</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return s.mk.bribes >= 5; }) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Paid 1–4 bribes</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return s.mk.bribes > 0 && s.mk.bribes < 5; }) + '</td></tr><tr><td>Bought the permit</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return s.k.permit; }) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Hired a helper</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return used(s, function (d) { return d.hp; }); }) + '</td></tr><tr><td>Took an angel investor</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return used(s, function (d) { return d.iv > 0; }); }) + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>Moved up a stand tier</td><td>' + who(function (s) { return used(s, function (d) { return d.tier > 1; }); }) + '</td></tr></table>');
  var dh = [[5, 14], [15, 29], [30, 59], [60, 99], [100, 1e9]].map(function (r) { return all.filter(function (s) { return s.days.length >= r[0] && s.days.length <= r[1]; }).length; });
  page('Part 6 · Attempts and persistence', 'Attempts and persistence', vis(svCols(['5–14', '15–29', '30–59', '60–99', '100+'], [dh], ['Students by days in graded attempt'], ['#2563eb'], function (v) { return v; }), '') +
    '<table><tr><th>Measure</th><th class="n">Students</th></tr><tr><td>Restarted at least once</td><td class="n">' + students.filter(function (s) { return s.restarts > 0; }).length + '</td></tr>' +
    '<tr><td>At least one failed game</td><td class="n">' + students.filter(function (s) { return s.failed > 0; }).length + '</td></tr><tr><td>Incomplete</td><td class="n">' + inc.length + '</td></tr><tr><td>Not graded</td><td class="n">' + ng.length + '</td></tr></table>' +
    (inc.length ? '<h3>Incomplete students: days still needed</h3><table><tr><th>Student</th><th class="n">Days so far</th><th class="n">Days needed</th><th class="n">Marks so far</th></tr>' + inc.map(function (s) { return '<tr><td>' + e(s.name) + '</td><td class="n">' + s.days.length + '</td><td class="n">' + (EVIDENCE_DAYS - s.days.length) + '</td><td class="n">' + s.mk.marks + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' : ''));
  var reason = function (s) { if (s.mk.inc || s.days.length < EVIDENCE_DAYS) return 'Stopped early'; if (s.mk.bribes >= 5) return 'Repeated bribery'; if (s.mk.avgP <= 0 || used(s, function (d) { return d.rj > 0; }) || s.mk.seized) return 'Losses and debt'; return 'Weak decisions'; };
  var act = { 'Stopped early': 'Play at least ' + EVIDENCE_DAYS + ' days in one game', 'Repeated bribery': 'Buy the permit at the first officer visit', 'Losses and debt': 'Avoid the moneylender; check break-even before opening', 'Weak decisions': 'Work on the weakest area in the report' };
  var att = all.filter(function (s) { return s.mk.inc || ['E', 'F'].indexOf(s.mk.gr.g) >= 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return reason(a) < reason(b) ? -1 : reason(a) > reason(b) ? 1 : a.mk.marks - b.mk.marks; });
  page('Part 7 · Students needing attention', 'Students needing attention', '<table><tr><th>Student</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th>Grade</th><th class="n">Days</th><th>Main reason</th><th>Most helpful action</th></tr>' +
    att.map(function (s) { var r = reason(s); return '<tr><td>' + e(s.name) + '</td><td class="n">' + s.mk.marks + '</td><td style="color:' + GCOL[s.mk.gr.g] + '"><b>' + s.mk.gr.g + '</b></td><td class="n">' + s.days.length + '</td><td>' + r + '</td><td class="sm">' + act[r] + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>');
  var impr = all.map(function (s) { var a = s.att.filter(function (x) { return x.mk; }); return a.length > 1 ? { s: s, d: r1(s.mk.attempt - a[0].mk.marks) } : null; }).filter(function (x) { return x && x.d > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.d - a.d; });
  page('Part 8 · Top performers and most improved', 'Top performers and most improved', '<h3>Top 10</h3><table><tr><th>#</th><th>Student</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th>Grade</th><th class="n">Days</th><th class="n">Profit a day</th></tr>' +
    gr.slice(0, 10).map(function (s) { return '<tr><td>' + s.rank + '</td><td>' + e(s.name) + '</td><td class="n">' + s.mk.marks + '</td><td><b style="color:' + GCOL[s.mk.gr.g] + '">' + s.mk.gr.g + '</b></td><td class="n">' + s.days.length + '</td><td class="n">' + m(s.mk.avgP) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' +
    '<h3>Most improved (first eligible attempt → best attempt)</h3>' + (impr.length ? '<table><tr><th>Student</th><th class="n">Improvement in marks</th></tr>' + impr.slice(0, 10).map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + e(x.s.name) + '</td><td class="n">+' + x.d + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' : '<p class="muted">No student has two graded attempts yet.</p>'));
  var qz = all.filter(function (s) { return s.quizPct != null; });
  if (qz.length) page('Part 9 · Quizzes', 'Quiz results', '<p>Quiz marks for ' + qz.length + ' students; class average ' + r1(avgOf(qz, function (s) { return s.quizPct; })) + '%.</p><table><tr><th>Student</th><th class="n">Quiz %</th></tr>' + qz.sort(function (a, b) { return b.quizPct - a.quizPct; }).map(function (s) { return '<tr><td>' + e(s.name) + '</td><td class="n">' + s.quizPct + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>');
  var reasons = {}; att.forEach(function (s) { var r = reason(s); reasons[r] = (reasons[r] || 0) + 1; });
  page('Part 10 · Teaching recommendations', 'Teaching recommendations', '<h3>Concepts to reteach</h3><ol>' + cn.slice(0, 3).map(function (c) { return '<li><b>' + e(c.nm) + '</b> (class average ' + c.avg + '; ' + c.low + ' students below 50). ' + e((NOTES[c.nm] || {}).d || '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>' +
    '<h3>Reminders before the deadline</h3><ul>' + Object.keys(reasons).sort(function (a, b) { return reasons[b] - reasons[a]; }).map(function (r) { return '<li>' + reasons[r] + ' student' + (reasons[r] > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' affected by <b>' + r.toLowerCase() + '</b>: ' + act[r] + '.</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' +
    '<h3>Suggested market events for the next session</h3><ul><li><b>Storm forecast</b> to practise risk management (close or scale down).</li><li><b>Festival</b> to practise production planning and dynamic pricing.</li><li><b>Rival promotion</b> to practise competitive response.</li></ul>' +
    '<p class="sm muted">For comparison with your other groups, use Compare sections in the admin panel.</p>');
  page('Appendix', 'Full marks table', '<table class="sm"><tr><th>#</th><th>Student</th><th>Player ID</th><th class="n">Days</th><th class="n">Attempts</th><th class="n">A</th><th class="n">B</th><th class="n">C</th><th class="n">Penalties</th><th class="n">Marks</th><th>Grade</th></tr>' +
    students.map(function (s) { var k2 = s.mk; return '<tr><td>' + (s.rank || '—') + '</td><td>' + e(s.name) + '</td><td>' + e(s.pid) + '</td><td class="n">' + s.days.length + '</td><td class="n">' + s.games + '</td><td class="n">' + (k2 ? k2.A : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + (k2 ? k2.B : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + (k2 ? k2.C : '') + '</td><td class="n">' + (k2 ? '−' + (k2.penB + k2.failPen) : '') + '</td><td class="n"><b>' + (k2 ? k2.marks : '—') + '</b></td><td>' + (k2 ? '<b style="color:' + GCOL[k2.gr.g] + '">' + k2.gr.g + '</b>' : 'Not graded') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>');
  return brandDoc(g.name + ' · group report', RN, cover(), H.join(''), who);
}
